> - Is employer-paid health insurance now compulsory and enforced for expatriate company workers, from what date, and how does a worker check he is covered?

**Work injury:** Work-injury and occupational-disease insurance under the Social Protection Law does not yet cover foreigners: Royal Decree 60/2025 put it back to five years after the law was issued in July 2023, that is July 2028 <sup>[107](#o107), [N46](#n46)</sup>. Until then, see [If you are injured or fall ill](#if-you-are-injured-or-fall-ill). MOL's leaflet says you are entitled to have your employer insure you against work accidents <sup>[98](#o98)</sup>.

<a id="your-passport"></a>

#### 3.11.5 Your passport

The employer may not keep your passport or personal documents without your written consent <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Breaking this rule is punishable by a fine of RO 500 to 1,000 for each worker affected, doubled for a repeat <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. If you hand in papers or certificates, the employer must give you a receipt <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. In practice confiscation is widespread; many workers have their passport taken by the employer or agency at the airport <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. Complain to MOL as under If something goes wrong <sup>[108](#o108)</sup>.

<a id="end-of-service-gratuity"></a>

#### 3.11.6 End-of-service gratuity

For workers outside the Social Protection Law's pension scheme (all foreigners), at least one month's basic wage for each year of service, part years pro rata, on your final basic wage <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Government dues and proven debts to the employer can be deducted from it <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. This rule lasts until the SPF's savings scheme for foreigners starts <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>: by law it must start no later than July 2027, and the employer will then pay 9% of your monthly basic wage into an SPF fund each month instead, paid out when your service in Oman ends <sup>[107](#o107), [N46](#n46), [109 (2023)](#o109)</sup>.

<a id="your-residence-card"></a>

### 3.12 Your residence card

Your right to live in Oman is your residence (on a work visa valid for 2 years from stamping, multi-entry), shown on the resident card the ROP (Royal Oman Police) issues <sup>[46](#o46), [51](#o51)</sup>. Getting the first card within 30 days is in [Step 8, item 2: The resident card within 30 days](#step-8-after-you-arrive-item-2) <sup>[51](#o51)</sup>.

<a id="validity-and-fees"></a>

#### 3.12.1 Validity and fees

Since November 2025 a resident card can run up to 10 years, but only for categories the ROP's Director General sets; the fees are in [Step 8, item 2: The resident card within 30 days](#step-8-after-you-arrive-item-2) <sup>[110](#o110)</sup>. MOL's leaflet says the work licence, ID card and residence fees are paid by the employer as long as you are legally on the job <sup>[98](#o98)</sup>. The work permit itself is renewed by the employer when it has less than 90 days left and you are in Oman; it costs RO 300, 250 or 200 by occupation class plus a 5% Job Security Fund fee <sup>[30](#o30)</sup>. Since Ministerial Decision 602/2025, work permits run 24 months to match the residence <sup>[111](#o111)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which category and validity does an ordinary Bangladeshi company worker get under the 10-year rule?

<a id="renewal"></a>

#### 3.12.2 Renewal

The Foreigners' Residence Law says to apply to renew your residence 15 days before it ends, and renewal can be refused without reasons <sup>[112](#o112)</sup>. Renew the card within 30 days of its expiry <sup>[110](#o110)</sup>. Renewal is in person, with the expired card, the renewal form, the worker data form and medical test, a letter from the employer and your valid passport <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>. ROP's visa page fines a late residence renewal RO 50 a month <sup>[46](#o46)</sup>. Failing without good reason to apply to renew on time can bring 7 days to 3 months in prison and/or RO 10 to 500 <sup>[112](#o112)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Who pays the RO 50-a-month fine when the employer let the residence lapse?

<a id="leaving-and-coming-back"></a>

#### 3.12.3 Leaving and coming back

You lose your right of residence if you stay outside Oman without an acceptable excuse for more than 6 months in a row, 8 months in total in a year, or 18 months in 3 years <sup>[112](#o112)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How does a worker stuck abroad longer (illness, family) apply for an exemption, and who applies?

<a id="overstaying"></a>

#### 3.12.4 Overstaying

Overstay fines are paid before you leave on the ROP's online Exit Penalty Payment service (fill in the form, pay online, print the receipt and show it at the exit desk) or on gov.om's Pay Fines Upon Departure service, which lists the overdue fee for residence visas as RO 20 a day <sup>[114](#o114), [115](#o115)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is the overstay fee on an expired residence really RO 20 a day (gov.om) rather than the RO 50 a month for late renewal on ROP's visa page, and from which day does it start?

<a id="what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported"></a>

### 3.13 What can get you jailed or deported

<a id="working-for-others-and-free-visas"></a>

#### 3.13.1 Working for others and "free visas"

You may work only for the employer that holds your work permit; another employer may use you only after MOL is told electronically and authorises it <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. A "free visa" (a work visa bought from a sponsor who has no real job for you, so you work for others) breaks this rule <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. A foreign worker who works without a permit or for someone other than his licensed employer faces 10 days to a month in prison and/or RO 1,000 to 2,000, loses his permit, and is deported at the employing party's expense and banned from entering Oman <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. The employer who used him faces the same penalty, doubled if the worker had entered illegally or left his own employer <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. The Minister can also deport a worker who breaks the labour rules, at the employer's expense, and ban his entry <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Workers who left their jobs without the employer's consent have been fined, deported or banned from coming back <sup>[N42 (2024)](#n42)</sup>.

<a id="leaving-your-job-absconding"></a>

#### 3.13.2 Leaving your job ("absconding")

Being absent without a good reason for more than 7 days in a row, or 10 separate days in a year (after a written warning at 5 days), lets the employer dismiss you without notice or gratuity <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. After 7 days it can also file a work-abandonment report: see If your employer reports you absent <sup>[31](#o31)</sup>.

<a id="late-papers-overstaying-and-false-papers"></a>

#### 3.13.3 Late papers, overstaying and false papers

Late renewal and overstay fines are under Your residence card <sup>[46](#o46), [114](#o114)</sup>. Giving false information or documents to get a visa or residence, entering Oman illegally, or not leaving after renewal is refused carries 1 month to 3 years in prison and/or RO 100 to 500 <sup>[112](#o112)</sup>. Using a false identity or forgery to get the job lets the employer dismiss you without notice or gratuity <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

<a id="strikes"></a>

#### 3.13.4 Strikes

Workers may form a labour union to defend their rights <sup>[102](#o102)</sup>. You may strike only if three-quarters of the general meeting of your workplace's labour union approve and the employer and MOL get 3 weeks' written notice with reasons and demands <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Strikes are banned in public and basic services, including oil facilities, refineries, ports, airports and public transport <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Strike days are unpaid <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Breaking the strike rules, or obstructing work during a strike, carries 1 to 6 months in prison and/or RO 500 to 3,000 <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

<a id="drugs"></a>

#### 3.13.5 Drugs

- Importing, exporting, producing or smuggling for trade: death or life imprisonment and at least RO 25,000 <sup>[N50 (1999)](#n50)</sup>.
- Importing, buying, making or holding for personal use: at least 1 year in prison and RO 500 to 3,000 <sup>[N50 (1999)](#n50)</sup>.
- Holding without intent to trade or use: up to 7 years and up to RO 5,000 <sup>[N50 (1999)](#n50)</sup>.
- Being under narcotics at work: dismissal without notice or gratuity <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

<a id="alcohol"></a>

#### 3.13.6 Alcohol

- Drinking in public, being drunk in public or causing a disturbance while drunk: 1 to 6 months and/or RO 100 to 500 <sup>[N51 (2018)](#n51)</sup>.
- Holding, making, bringing in or dealing in alcohol without a licence: 6 months to 3 years and/or RO 300 to 1,000 <sup>[N51 (2018)](#n51)</sup>.
- Being drunk at work, or an act against public morals: dismissal without notice or gratuity <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

<a id="hundi-informal-money-transfer"></a>

#### 3.13.7 Hundi (informal money transfer)

In August 2026 police in Al Buraimi arrested an Asian man who sent money home for expatriate workers through unlicensed apps <sup>[N52](#n52)</sup>. Send money only through banks and licensed exchange houses: see [Sending money home](#sending-money-home).

> **Need to know**
> - What penalty does the sender (not the operator) face for using hundi in Oman?

<a id="bribes"></a>

#### 3.13.8 Bribes

Since June 2026 a private-sector worker who asks for or takes a reward to do (or not do) his job faces 1 to 3 years in prison and a fine at least equal to the amount <sup>[N41](#n41)</sup>.

<a id="crimes-and-deportation"></a>

#### 3.13.9 Crimes and deportation

A foreigner finally convicted of a felony, or of a misdemeanour where the court orders it, is deported after serving the sentence <sup>[112](#o112)</sup>. A final conviction for a felony, a crime of honour or trust, or a workplace offence also lets the employer dismiss you without notice or gratuity <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. The ROP's Inspector General can cancel a residence and deport someone who breaches public order or morals or has no visible lawful means of living <sup>[112](#o112)</sup>.

<a id="bans-on-coming-back"></a>

#### 3.13.10 Bans on coming back

Anyone removed or deported goes on the list of people not wanted in Oman, and can get a visa again only after 2 years and with the Inspector General's special permission <sup>[112](#o112)</sup>. An employer that used a worker illegally can itself be banned from recruiting for up to 2 years <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

<a id="if-something-goes-wrong"></a>

### 3.14 If something goes wrong

<a id="if-you-arent-paid"></a>

#### 3.14.1 If you aren't paid

Your wage is late if it is not in your bank account within 3 days of the end of the pay period <sup>[8](#o8), [103 (2024)](#o103)</sup>. If it is unpaid for more than 2 months in a row, you may leave the job without notice and keep all your rights, including the gratuity, after telling the employer <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Every claim under the Labour Law lapses one year after it falls due, so act early <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Workers' lawsuits under the Labour Law are free of court fees <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

1. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-1"></a>**Ask first, in writing.** MOL's leaflet says to ask your manager, and if he does not answer, to go to MOL <sup>[98](#o98)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-2"></a>**Collect proof.** Keep your contract, resident card, bank statements showing what was and was not paid, and any messages. The employer must prove it paid <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-3"></a>**Complain to MOL.** Use MOL's "Record Labor Complaint" service (on gov.om or the MOL website): no documents and no fee are listed; you can track the complaint and ask to close it <sup>[108](#o108)</sup>. MOL's call centre is 80077000 <sup>[102](#o102)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-4"></a>**The settlement stage.** Both sides are called; MOL tries to settle within 30 days of your request <sup>[108](#o108), [102](#o102)</sup>. A settlement is written in a report signed by both sides, the MOL officer and a judge assigned to MOL, and can be enforced like a court order <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
5. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-5"></a>**Court.** If there is no settlement, MOL sends the dispute to the court within 7 days of the last session, with a memo, your contract and the evidence <sup>[102](#o102), [8](#o8)</sup>. A lawsuit filed straight at the court without the MOL step is not admitted <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
6. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-6"></a>**Stay for the case.** A worker who files a lawsuit for his dues may stay in Oman until it is decided <sup>[N29 (2023)](#n29)</sup>.
7. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-7"></a>**Dismissed?** Complain to MOL within 30 days of being told <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Dismissal for complaining is unfair dismissal, unless the complaint was vexatious, and the court then orders reinstatement or 3 to 12 months' gross wage, plus gratuity, notice pay and insurance contributions <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
8. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-8"></a>**The Embassy.** See Help from the Bangladesh Embassy below.

In practice, observers report that MOL remedies are unpredictable and often slow, and that officials commonly side with employers <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long do MOL settlement and the labour court take in practice for a Bangladeshi worker?
> - Is there a fund that pays workers when an employer cannot, as Qatar's WSIF (Workers' Support and Insurance Fund) does?

<a id="if-you-are-injured-or-fall-ill"></a>

#### 3.14.2 If you are injured or fall ill

Until SPF work-injury cover reaches foreigners (July 2028), the employer pays for hospital treatment if you have no health insurance <sup>[107](#o107), [8](#o8)</sup>. Sick pay is under Your rights at work <sup>[102](#o102)</sup>. If there is a serious danger the employer knows about and ignores, you may leave without notice and keep your rights <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Compensation, bringing an injured worker home and help from the Welfare Board are under If a worker dies, is injured or loses the job.

> **Need to know**
> - Under what rule does a foreign worker injured at work claim compensation from the employer in 2026?

<a id="if-you-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-kept"></a>

#### 3.14.3 If you are abused or your passport is kept

1. <a id="if-you-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-kept-item-1"></a>**MOL** for labour violations (complaint above) <sup>[108](#o108)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-you-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-kept-item-2"></a>**The ROP** for assault or crime (emergency number 9999); ROP also runs the trafficking hotline, MOL a labour-violation hotline and the Ministry of Social Development (MOSD) a shelter hotline, all 24 hours, with Bengali speakers available on request <sup>[34](#o34), [N44](#n44)</sup>. Observers report unanswered calls and language problems <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-you-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-kept-item-3"></a>**OHRC (Oman Human Rights Commission)**, a government-funded commission that reports to the Sultan <sup>[N42 (2024)](#n42)</sup>: Al-Fardan Building, 4th Floor, Al Azaiba, Muscat, Sunday to Thursday 8:00 am to 2:30 pm; phone 24218900; WhatsApp 72221966; voice message on toll-free 1970 if you cannot write in Arabic or English; or the online form at ohrc.om <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>. Bring or attach your documents; you get a reference number to follow up <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>. OHRC cannot order anyone to act; it tries to settle cases with the authorities <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-you-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-kept-item-4"></a>**Shelter.** MOSD runs a government shelter for up to 50 women, 30 men and 20 children who are victims of trafficking or abuse <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. It admits someone with an absconding charge only if he or she had already complained against the employer, and residents may go out only with a chaperone <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. Shelters for women are under Work visa: domestic worker.

Assault by the employer or a superior, or an immoral act against you, also lets you leave without notice and keep your rights <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What are the ROP trafficking hotline and MOL labour-violation hotline numbers?

<a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding"></a>

#### 3.14.4 If your employer reports you absent ("absconding")

**What it is:** A work-abandonment ("desertion") report is filed by the employer on MOL's "File Desertion Report" service when a foreign worker has been absent for over 7 days; it protects the employer from legal responsibility and fines for the worker <sup>[31](#o31)</sup>. The employer must attach your pay statements and pays the price of your ticket home, plus work-permit fees if the permit has expired; it cannot be filed once your status is "departed" <sup>[31](#o31)</sup>. Once the report has been approved for more than 30 days, the employer no longer has to pay you through WPS <sup>[103 (2024)](#o103)</sup>.

**What it can lead to:** An absconding charge is an "immigration block" that stops you leaving Oman until cleared <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. Workers who fled abusive employers have been arrested for absconding <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. Working for someone else meanwhile is the "free visa" offence above <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

**Checking and clearing it:**

1. <a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding-item-1"></a>**Check.** Log in to MOL's "Check Your Employment Record in the Private Sector" service (free, no documents) to see your record <sup>[99](#o99)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding-item-2"></a>**Appeal.** Use MOL's "Appeal Against Desertion Report for Individuals" service, with supporting documents (pay slips, your complaint, proof you were at work), once the report has been approved <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding-item-3"></a>**Complain first if you can.** If you left because of unpaid wages or abuse, file an MOL complaint before or as soon as you leave; the government shelter only takes people with absconding charges who had complained first <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding-item-4"></a>**Ask the Embassy's labour wing** (below).

MOL's 2025 amnesty let workers and employers cancel work-abandonment reports without fines until 31 December 2025, and MOL said no applications would be taken after that date <sup>[N53](#n53)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What does the employment-record service actually show: does it show a desertion report?
> - How long does an appeal take, and what happens to the worker while it runs?

<a id="help-from-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-muscat"></a>

#### 3.14.5 Help from the Bangladesh Embassy in Muscat

- **Labour wing:** Counsellor (Labour) +968 2469 8440, mobile +968 9941 3132, counselormuscat@probashi.gov.bd; Second Secretary (Labour) +968 2469 4798, mobile +968 9524 1859, fsmuscat@probashi.gov.bd <sup>[117](#o117)</sup>.
- **Workers' problems landline:** +968 24603514 <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>.
- **Expatriate Service Cell** (labour welfare: dead bodies, legal help, out passes, accidents, illness): WhatsApp +968 91980621 (text or voice message only, 24 hours), consularmuscat@gmail.com, or amardutabashmuscat.org <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>.
- **Emergency hotline:** +968 91997852, voice calls only for real emergencies such as an accident that has just happened <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>.
- **Address:** Villa 4207, Way 3052, Shatti Al Qurum, Muscat; PO Box 3959, PC 112, Ruwi; +968 2469 8336; mission.muscat@mofa.gov.bd; book appointments at amardutabashmuscat.org <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does the labour wing go with workers to MOL hearings or pay for a lawyer, and how often?
> - Does the Embassy run a toll-free line or open house, as India's embassy does (24/7 toll-free 80071234 and a monthly open house) <sup>[N54](#n54)</sup>?

<a id="changing-employer-or-leaving"></a>

### 3.15 Changing employer or leaving

<a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer"></a>

#### 3.15.1 If you want to change employer

**No permission needed at the end of the contract:** Since 1 January 2021 your residence can move to another employer licensed to recruit once you show that your contract has ended or been terminated and the government has approved the new contract; your old employer's NOC (no-objection certificate) is no longer needed, and your family's residence moves with you if they meet the residence conditions <sup>[118 (2020)](#o118), [119 (2021)](#o119)</sup>. MOL still has to approve the transfer <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. Changing before the contract ends still needs the employer's agreement, and some employers demand illegal "release fees" for it <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>.

**Notice:** On an open-ended contract either side may end it for a legitimate reason with 30 days' written notice (15 days if you are not paid monthly); whoever skips notice pays the wage for the period <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. During the employer's notice you get 10 paid hours a week to look for a job <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

**How the transfer is done:**

1. <a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer-item-1"></a>**The new employer** must have a vacant recruitment licence for your job; you must be actively employed <sup>[33](#o33)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer-item-2"></a>**MOL transfer:** the request is registered on MOL's "Transfer Non-Omani Workers Services", approved by the previous sponsor and then the new one, and the form is issued; the processing fee is RO 5 <sup>[33](#o33)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer-item-3"></a>**ROP change of sponsor:** after the MOL transfer, the residence moves on the ROP's "Change the Employer (Sponsor)" service with your passport copy and a photo, for RO 20 <sup>[120](#o120)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer-item-4"></a>**Check** on MOL's employment-record service that the new employer shows <sup>[99](#o99)</sup>.

The law does not say how a worker who left after two months unpaid finds a new job, or give him a grace period <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - If the old employer refuses the transfer at the end of the contract, does MOL approve it anyway, and how?

<a id="resigning"></a>

#### 3.15.2 Resigning

You can resign online on MOL's website: log in with your civil ID card or e-authenticated phone number, click "Add New Resignation Request", and give your details, the establishment's and the contract's; the resignation date can be at most 30 days ahead <sup>[32](#o32)</sup>. Your status then becomes "job seeker", and you can print the contract termination form; a pending resignation can be cancelled only with the employer's approval <sup>[32](#o32)</sup>. MOL's leaflet warns that resigning on the spot can leave you in an illegal situation <sup>[98](#o98)</sup>.

<a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job"></a>

#### 3.15.3 Leaving at the end of the job

1. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-1"></a>**Collect what you are owed.** All wages and dues are due as soon as the job ends (within 7 days if you left on your own) <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. That includes the gratuity and cash for leave not taken <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. On request the employer must give you a free end-of-service certificate and return your papers <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. MOL's employment-record service also gives an end-of-service form <sup>[99](#o99)</sup>.
2. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-2"></a>**Your ticket home.** The employer must send you back to your country within 60 days of the contract ending, and give you on request a release from liability <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. If you refuse to travel, MOL sends you back at the employer's cost <sup>[N29 (2023)](#n29)</sup>.
3. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-3"></a>**Clear fines and blocks.** Overstay fines are paid on ROP's Exit Penalty Payment service <sup>[114](#o114)</sup>. An absconding charge, unpaid fines, or not being removed from the employer's sponsorship blocks departure <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>.
4. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-4"></a>**If you are still owed money,** file at MOL before you go; you may stay until your court case is decided <sup>[N29 (2023)](#n29)</sup>.

**Employer tricks at the end of the job:**

- **Release fees:** demanding money to let you go home or change job; families and labour-sending governments have paid employers up to US$3,900 per worker to get a worker home <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. Charging workers for recruitment is illegal <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
- **Keeping you on the sponsorship** so you cannot leave until you pay <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>.
- **A false absconding report** to avoid paying dues: see If your employer reports you absent <sup>[31](#o31)</sup>.
- **A signed waiver:** any release or waiver of your legal rights is void <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

<a id="going-home-on-leave"></a>

### 3.16 Going home on leave

Your annual leave is under Your rights at work <sup>[102](#o102)</sup>. You are entitled to a return ticket to Bangladesh for the leave agreed in your contract <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Do not stay away more than 6 months in a row, or your residence lapses <sup>[112](#o112)</sup>. Make sure your resident card and passport will still be valid when you come back <sup>[110](#o110), [98](#o98)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - If the contract says nothing about leave tickets, must the employer still pay one?

<a id="bringing-your-family"></a>

### 3.17 Bringing your family

A worker can bring a spouse and children under 21 on the family joining visa; who qualifies (the salary threshold is unsettled), the papers and the steps are under Family joining visa <sup>[121](#o121), [122](#o122), [N55 (2023)](#n55)</sup>.

<a id="work-visa-domestic-worker"></a>

## 4 Work visa: domestic worker

Domestic work in Oman covers 12 jobs done for a household: housemaid, nanny, private (house) driver, gardener, farm worker, residential building guard, home health assistant, private nurse, home cook, camel herder, livestock herder and horse groom <sup>[123](#o123), [N40](#n40)</sup>. Since 13 October 2025, the day after its publication in the Official Gazette of 12 October, they have been governed by Ministerial Decision 574/2025, the Regulation on the Work of Domestic Workers and Those in Their Position, which replaced the 2004 rules (Ministerial Decision 189/2004) <sup>[123](#o123), [N40](#n40)</sup>. The Labour Law (Royal Decree 53/2023) does not apply to work governed by special rules, so domestic workers' rights come from MD 574/2025, which uses the Labour Law only for how disputes are settled <sup>[8](#o8), [123](#o123)</sup>. The rights it sets are a floor: any term that cuts or waives them is void <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.

**Is the route open from Bangladesh?** No, in practice. The Royal Oman Police (ROP) suspended all visa categories for Bangladeshis except official ones in October 2023, and the categories it exempted in June 2024 (family and GCC-resident visit visas, engineers, doctors, nurses, teachers, accountants, investors and high-income tourists) do not include domestic workers <sup>[N12 (2024)](#n12)</sup>. BMET (the Bureau of Manpower, Employment and Training) cleared 6,636 women for Oman in 2023, 560 of them in November and December, after the suspension; then 56 in 2024, 5 in 2025 and 3 from 1 January to 8 November 2026 <sup>[124 (2023)](#o124), [125 (2023)](#o125), [126 (2024)](#o126), [127](#o127), [128](#o128)</sup>. In August 2026 the Omani Agricultural Association's Al Dhahirah branch opened applications from its registered farmers to recruit Bangladeshi farm workers, under approvals from the Ministry of Labour and the ROP; a visa-services office owner said 5,000 workers had been approved across Oman <sup>[N4](#n4)</sup>. Farm work is one of the 12 domestic-work jobs; that opening is covered under the company work visa <sup>[123](#o123), [N40](#n40)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Has Oman's Ministry of Labour or the ROP issued any Bangladeshi housemaid or house-driver visas since June 2024, and under which exemption?
> - Do the reopened farm-worker visas come under MD 574/2025 (domestic workers) or the Labour Law?

**How many went before the suspension.** Oman was the second-largest destination for Bangladeshi women after Saudi Arabia: 52,626 women went from 1991 to 2015, 16,980 in 2015 alone, 17,654 in 2019, 10,035 in 2021 and 15,759 in 2022 <sup>[N56 (2016)](#n56), [N9 (2021)](#n9), [N57 (2023)](#n57)</sup>. Of the 6,636 women cleared in 2023, 2,936 went as House Keeper, 1,702 as Servant, 1,199 as House Worker and 357 as Housekeeping Attendant <sup>[129 (2023)](#o129)</sup>. The districts sending most were Habiganj (561), Sunamganj (482), Dhaka (409) and Moulvibazar (329) <sup>[124 (2023)](#o124)</sup>.

**Men as domestic workers.** Men go as house drivers, cooks, gardeners and "servants" under the same regulation <sup>[123](#o123), [N40](#n40)</sup>. In 2023 BMET cleared 4,484 people for Oman as Servant (1,702 of them women), 3,662 as Private Service, 1,294 as Cook, 330 as Driver and 74 as Gardener, out of 127,822 in all <sup>[130 (2023)](#o130), [131 (2023)](#o131)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does Bangladesh set an age limit, test or licence rule for men going to Oman as house drivers?
> - What did a house-driver visa for Oman sell for in Bangladesh before October 2023?

<a id="who-pays-what-1"></a>

### 4.1 Who pays what

**In Oman, the household pays** <sup>[132](#o132), [N31](#n31), [133](#o133), [134](#o134), [123](#o123)</sup>:
- the Ministry of Labour (MOL) permit, at the rates in force since 15 February 2026: RO 101 per worker for its first 1 to 3 housemaids, nannies, private drivers, gardeners, home health assistants or private nurses, and RO 141 from the 4th; for farm workers, camel and livestock herders, horse grooms and residential building workers, RO 141 per worker for the first 1 to 3 in each occupation and RO 241 from the 4th
- the ROP visa: RO 20, not refundable
- the recruitment office's service fee, whose amount MD 390/2026 leaves to a later decision of the Minister of Labour <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>
- the worker's health insurance

Households with a disabled or elderly member who needs care, or on the Social Protection Fund's family income benefit, pay no permit fee for one worker <sup>[132](#o132)</sup>.

**The worker pays nothing in Oman.** The employer may take no money from the worker for recruiting, employing or transferring her, and a recruitment office may take no fee from her <sup>[123](#o123), [135](#o135)</sup>. A deduction from wages can only be for damage she caused, a court or official order, or an interest-free loan agreed in writing, and never more than a quarter of the wage <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.

**The worker pays nothing in Bangladesh.** An agency approved to send women domestic workers may take no migration cost from her <sup>[136](#o136)</sup>. The Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare's 2016 guideline for Oman-bound housemaids also made their migration free, the employer paying <sup>[N56 (2016)](#n56)</sup>.

**In practice,** domestic workers still paid fees to home-country agents linked to Omani offices, and those who tried to leave were often made to pay a "release fee" to reimburse the employer or agency <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. From 1 January 2027, a recruitment office may claim its recruitment costs from a worker who leaves the employer without a legal reason or assaults the family, up to the amount it refunded the employer <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What does an Omani household pay a recruitment office for a Bangladeshi housemaid, and has the Minister issued the decision setting the service fee under MD 390/2026?
> - What did a Bangladeshi woman really pay an agency or broker to go to Oman as a housemaid in 2023?

<a id="how-a-household-in-oman-recruits"></a>

### 4.2 How a household in Oman recruits

1. <a id="how-a-household-in-oman-recruits-item-1"></a>**MOL work authorisation.** The household needs a licence from MOL to bring a worker <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>. It is valid 24 months and cannot be renewed; the worker's work permit is for up to 24 months and renewable <sup>[132](#o132)</sup>.
2. <a id="how-a-household-in-oman-recruits-item-2"></a>**The ROP visa.** On Gov.om, Oman's government services portal, the employer applies for the "House worker Visa" with a copy of the worker's passport, a digital photo, the MOL work permit and a copy of the medical; the worker must be 21 or older with a passport valid 6 months, and the residence lasts up to 2 years, renewable through MOL <sup>[134](#o134)</sup>.
3. <a id="how-a-household-in-oman-recruits-item-3"></a>**Through a recruitment office.** From 1 January 2027, MD 390/2026 makes recruitment of domestic workers lawful only through a licensed recruitment office, and recruiting a domestic worker outside one is fined RO 100 per worker <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>. The office must be Omani-owned, lodge a RO 5,000 guarantee with MOL and pay RO 300 for a 2-year licence <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>. MOL keeps a register of offices and may publish it on its website <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>.
4. <a id="how-a-household-in-oman-recruits-item-4"></a>**The job offer and service contract.** Before the worker travels, the office must give her a written job offer signed by the employer, on MOL's model form, stating the duties, conditions and wage; the office's contract with the employer is approved by MOL only after she has agreed to that offer <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>. The contract is made in 3 copies, one for MOL, with the job offer attached <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>.
5. <a id="how-a-household-in-oman-recruits-item-5"></a>**Arrival within 45 days.** The office must bring the worker within 45 days of the contract, insure the contract (including her ticket home), and have her medically checked before she enters Oman <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>.
6. <a id="how-a-household-in-oman-recruits-item-6"></a>**After arrival.** The employer collects her within 48 hours, and within 30 days finishes her residence card, medical and a work contract with her <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>. The employer registers the work contract on MOL's electronic system <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.

**The refund guarantee.** For 2 years the office must refund the employer if she is returned for listed reasons: in full within 90 days, then pro rata <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>. The reasons include refusing to work or leaving without a legal reason, illness, and pregnancy found before she starts, in which case the office sends her home within 10 days <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>. The employer loses the refund if he lied about the terms, left her unpaid for more than 2 months in a row, or he or his family abused or harassed her <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>.

**Temporary permits.** From 2027 an office may also bring workers before a household is found, on a 180-day temporary permit costing RO 26 <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>. While she waits it pays her 60% of the offered wage for the first 60 days and 100% after that, with free lodging, food and health care <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>. She can be tried for 7 days in a home, for at most 28 days and 4 households in all, and gets a free ticket home if her 180 days run out or her trials pass 28 days <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Under the 2011 office regulation in force until 31 December 2026, may an Omani household still hire a Bangladeshi housemaid directly, without an office?
> - Where does MOL publish its list of licensed recruitment offices?

<a id="step-1-check-the-route-is-really-open"></a>

### 4.3 Step 1: Check the route is really open

1. <a id="step-1-check-the-route-is-really-open-item-1"></a>**Check BMET's numbers before paying anyone.** BMET cleared only 5 women for Oman in 2025 and 3 in 2026 to 8 November, so an offer of a housemaid job in Oman is not a normal open route <sup>[127](#o127), [128](#o128)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-1-check-the-route-is-really-open-item-2"></a>**Check the visa type is not visit or tourist.** Domestic worker visas are not among the categories open to Bangladeshis since June 2024 <sup>[N12 (2024)](#n12)</sup>.

<a id="step-2-check-you-qualify"></a>

### 4.4 Step 2: Check you qualify

1. <a id="step-2-check-you-qualify-item-1"></a>**Oman's age limit:** at least 21 when the visa is applied for, for women and men <sup>[123](#o123), [134](#o134)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-check-you-qualify-item-2"></a>**Bangladesh's rules for women:** 25 to 45 when you enrol in the housekeeping course (returnees up to 50), class 5 passed (can be relaxed), and not pregnant, with your youngest child at least 6 and no contagious or incurable disease <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>. BMET clearance also needs proof you are 25 to 45 <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-2-check-you-qualify-item-3"></a>**Your passport** must be valid at least 6 months <sup>[134](#o134)</sup>.

<a id="step-3-women-take-the-housekeeping-course"></a>

### 4.5 Step 3: Women: take the housekeeping course

It is a residential one-month course (24 working days) at a BMET Technical Training Centre (TTC) <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>.

You'll need <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>:
- proof that you passed class 5
- a no-objection letter from your legal guardian
- a copy of your birth certificate, NID (national ID card) or passport

1. Apply online when BMET posts the admission notice, pay the set fee and choose the centre and date <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>.
2. Bring the originals to the interview, where staff check you can read and write, and enrol in person <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>.
3. Attend 90% of sessions and score 70% in the final 100-mark test <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>. The course teaches how to reach the police and the Bangladesh embassy in the destination country <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>.
4. Download and print the certificate online <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>. It is valid 2 years for a first departure; at 2 to 3 years old you take a 3-day refresher, and after 3 years the full course again <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the housekeeping course fee in taka?

<a id="step-4-go-only-through-an-approved-agency"></a>

### 4.6 Step 4: Go only through an approved agency

1. <a id="step-4-go-only-through-an-approved-agency-item-1"></a>**Women: use one of the 21 approved agencies.** On 1 September 2025 the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare approved 21 licensed agencies to send women domestic workers abroad <sup>[136](#o136)</sup>. Each deposits Tk 15 lakh with BMET, keeps at least one woman on its office staff, takes no migration cost from you and signs an undertaking that its licence can be cancelled if you are abused at work <sup>[136](#o136)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-4-go-only-through-an-approved-agency-item-2"></a>**The 2016 guideline for Oman.** The Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare's guideline for Oman-bound housemaids, effective 16 October 2016, also has your legal guardian and the agency sign affidavits on Tk 300 stamp paper <sup>[N56 (2016)](#n56)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-4-go-only-through-an-approved-agency-item-3"></a>**If an agency asks you for money,** complain to the BMET Director General with proof at BMET's complaint cell; it is free and decided within 30 working days <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which of the 21 approved agencies have Omani partner offices?

<a id="step-5-check-the-job-the-employer-and-the-contract"></a>

### 4.7 Step 5: Check the job, the employer and the contract

1. <a id="step-5-check-the-job-the-employer-and-the-contract-item-1"></a>**The job offer.** Ask for the written offer signed by the employer, with the duties, conditions and wage, before you agree <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-5-check-the-job-the-employer-and-the-contract-item-2"></a>**The wage.** Bangladesh's minimum for Oman housemaids was set at RO 90 a month (Tk 18,000) in 2016 and agreed with Oman, and the sponsor had to earn at least RO 1,000 a month <sup>[N56 (2016)](#n56), [N9 (2021)](#n9)</sup>. Agents have been heard advising employers to pay first-timers RO 80 <sup>[N9 (2021)](#n9)</sup>. India, by comparison, makes an Omani employer of an Indian housemaid lodge a RO 1,100 bank guarantee <sup>[N58](#n58)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-5-check-the-job-the-employer-and-the-contract-item-3"></a>**The contract.** It must be written in Arabic in two copies, one for you, and may have a copy in another language <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>. It must state <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>:
   - both sides' details
   - start date and probation
   - the work and where it is done
   - hours and rest
   - the monthly wage and its payment date
4. <a id="step-5-check-the-job-the-employer-and-the-contract-item-4"></a>**Embassy attestation.** Under the rules reported in December 2016 for hiring Bangladeshi housemaids from January 2017, the contract between you and the employer had to be attested by the Bangladesh Embassy in Oman, and the employer paid your travel and insurance <sup>[N59 (2016)](#n59)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is the 2016 Oman housemaid guideline (RO 90 minimum, RO 1,000 sponsor income, embassy attestation) still in force, and has the minimum been raised?
> - What does the Embassy in Muscat charge to attest a housemaid contract, and what papers does the sponsor bring?

<a id="step-6-the-visa-and-the-medical"></a>

### 4.8 Step 6: The visa and the medical

1. <a id="step-6-the-visa-and-the-medical-item-1"></a>**The employer gets the visa** on Gov.om with your passport copy, photo, MOL work permit and medical copy; the fee is RO 20 <sup>[134](#o134)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-6-the-visa-and-the-medical-item-2"></a>**The medical** is done before you enter Oman, arranged by the recruitment office <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Where in Bangladesh do Oman-bound domestic workers do their pre-departure medical?
> - How can a worker check an Omani visa is real and is a domestic-worker visa?

<a id="step-7-bmet-clearance-and-a-loan"></a>

### 4.9 Step 7: BMET clearance and a loan

1. <a id="step-7-bmet-clearance-and-a-loan-item-1"></a>**BMET clearance.** The agency applies; BMET's emigration branch checks the papers and clears you once the visa is issued, within 3 working days <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>. For a woman domestic worker the agency also submits proof you are 25 to 45 and your guardian's no-objection letter <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-7-bmet-clearance-and-a-loan-item-2"></a>**A loan if you need one.** Probashi Kallyan Bank (PKB) lends women migrants up to Tk 3 lakh at 9% simple interest over up to 3 years <sup>[96](#o96)</sup>.

<a id="step-8-after-you-arrive-1"></a>

### 4.10 Step 8: After you arrive

1. <a id="step-8-after-you-arrive-1-item-1"></a>**The first days.** The office houses and feeds you for the first 48 hours; the employer then takes you home and, within 30 days, completes your residence card, medical and work contract <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-8-after-you-arrive-1-item-2"></a>**Your passport.** Many workers say the employer or agency took their passport at the airport <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. The law lets them hold it only with your written consent <sup>[123](#o123), [135](#o135)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-8-after-you-arrive-1-item-3"></a>**Your work permit.** You may not start work before MOL issues it <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-8-after-you-arrive-1-item-4"></a>**Probation** is at most 15 days; either side can end the contract in it with 7 days' notice <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.

<a id="scams-1"></a>

### 4.11 Scams

<a id="done-to-workers-1"></a>

#### 4.11.1 Done to workers

**Sold through the UAE:** Women recruited for UAE jobs have been taken across the Al Ain–Buraimi border into Oman, where embassies cannot track them <sup>[N8 (2016)](#n8)</sup>. Agencies in Al Ain and Buraimi supply Omani employers who come on Fridays <sup>[N8 (2016)](#n8)</sup>. One Bangladeshi woman who paid an agent RO 300 for a UAE job was "sold" to an Omani man who demanded RO 1,560 before he would let her go <sup>[N8 (2016)](#n8)</sup>. Traffickers still recruit with promises of shop jobs in Dubai and then move workers to Oman, and some workers come on tourist visas or via the UAE, outside their own government's checks <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>.

**A shop job that turns out to be housework:** A woman who paid Tk 100,000 for a customer-service job in Oman found her visa said domestic worker <sup>[N8 (2016)](#n8)</sup>. Check the job title on the visa and job offer before you pay or fly <sup>[135](#o135), [N8 (2016)](#n8)</sup>.

**Social-media brokers:** Unregulated intermediaries on social media offer Omani families cheap domestic workers at a fraction of agency prices <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. From 2027, only a licensed office may recruit a domestic worker <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>.

**"Release fees":** Employers and agents demand money before letting a worker leave or change employer; one agent told a Bangladeshi woman she owed him RO 200 <sup>[N44](#n44), [N8 (2016)](#n8)</sup>. The employer may take no money from you for recruiting or transferring you <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.

**"Oman is open":** Offers of Oman housemaid visas are not a sign the route has reopened: only 3 women were cleared for Oman in 2026 to 8 November <sup>[128](#o128)</sup>.

<a id="tried-by-workers-1"></a>

#### 4.11.2 Tried by workers

Women who are under 25 or over 45 cannot get BMET clearance as domestic workers <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>. Your employer may not let you work for yourself or for anyone else <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.

<a id="your-rights-at-work-1"></a>

### 4.12 Your rights at work

<a id="care-and-dignity"></a>

#### 4.12.1 Care and dignity

The employer must treat you well, protect your dignity, safety and health, give you food and suitable accommodation, and not give you dangerous work <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>. Forced labour, violence and harassment are banned <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>. Your room must be ventilated, lit and air-conditioned, with good bedding, a wardrobe and a clean toilet <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>. If you live outside the home, the employer gives you free transport <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>. The employer pays your health insurance <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.

<a id="hours-rest-and-leave-1"></a>

#### 4.12.2 Hours, rest and leave

- at most 12 hours of work a day, with at least 8 hours' rest a day <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>
- overtime of at most 2 hours a day, agreed in writing and paid <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>
- at least 1 paid day off a week; work on it only by written agreement, for an extra day's pay or another day off <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>
- paid sick leave of up to 30 days a year with a medical certificate, and no work while sick <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>
- at least 21 days' paid annual leave a year, paid out if unused when you leave <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>

<a id="pay"></a>

#### 4.12.3 Pay

You are paid in rials within 7 days of the due date in your contract, against your signature in a register or into a bank account <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>. MOL may set a system employers must use <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.

<a id="end-of-service-pay"></a>

#### 4.12.4 End-of-service pay

Half a month's wage for each year, counted from 13 October 2025 when MD 574/2025 took effect, and nothing if you served under 2 years <sup>[123](#o123), [N40](#n40)</sup>.

<a id="the-passport-and-the-phone"></a>

#### 4.12.5 The passport and the phone

The employer and the office may keep your passport only with your written consent <sup>[123](#o123), [135](#o135)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does any Omani rule protect a domestic worker's right to keep and use her own phone?

<a id="penalties-and-practice"></a>

#### 4.12.6 Penalties and practice

An employer who breaks the regulation can be fined RO 50 to RO 500 per breach, for each worker affected <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>. MOL inspectors may interview you, call you to MOL, and visit the home with the Public Prosecution's permission <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>. In practice, about a quarter of the 59 domestic workers HRW interviewed in 2015 said employers had physically or sexually abused them <sup>[N8 (2016)](#n8)</sup>.

<a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1"></a>

### 4.13 If something goes wrong

<a id="complain-in-this-order"></a>

#### 4.13.1 Complain in this order

1. <a id="complain-in-this-order-item-1"></a>**MOL.** File a free "Record Labor Complaint" on Gov.om, with no documents; both sides are called, then it goes to a judge and court <sup>[108](#o108)</sup>. MOL tries to settle within 30 days, then refers it to court within 7 days; you pay no court fees <sup>[8](#o8), [123](#o123)</sup>. Claim within a year of the money falling due <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. The recruitment office can also report your employer to MOL for you <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>. MOL's call centre is 80077000 <sup>[138](#o138), [98](#o98)</sup>.
2. <a id="complain-in-this-order-item-2"></a>**The Bangladesh Embassy in Muscat** <sup>[100](#o100), [117](#o117)</sup>:
   - workers' problems: +968 24603514, 8:30 am to 4:30 pm
   - labour wing Counsellor: +968 2469 8440, +968 9941 3132, counselormuscat@probashi.gov.bd
   - labour wing Second Secretary: +968 2469 4798, +968 9524 1859, fsmuscat@probashi.gov.bd
   - emergency hotline, voice calls for real emergencies only, 24 hours: +968 91997852
3. <a id="complain-in-this-order-item-3"></a>**From Bangladesh.** Your family can report harassment to the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare at aswelfare@probashi.gov.bd with your passport, smart card and residence permit copies and the agency's details; free, 7 working days <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which email reaches the Muscat labour wing today: counselormuscat@probashi.gov.bd or clomanbd@mail.com?

<a id="leaving-an-abusive-home"></a>

#### 4.13.2 Leaving an abusive home

You may end the contract at once, keeping all your rights including end-of-service pay, after telling the employer, if <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>:
- you are not paid in full for more than 2 months in a row, or the employer breaks the rules or contract
- the employer or his representative cheated you on the terms when you were hired
- the employer or a family member assaults you or commits an immoral act towards you
- there is a serious danger to your safety or health that the employer knows of and has not removed

In the past, police returned some women to employers or agents, and some were beaten after <sup>[N60 (2016)](#n60)</sup>. Domestic workers who flee are also at risk of sex trafficking <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. An employer can report you absent after 7 days through MOL's "File Desertion Report" service, which can lead to deportation, a work ban or a criminal complaint <sup>[31](#o31), [N60 (2016)](#n60)</sup>.

<a id="shelters"></a>

#### 4.13.3 Shelters

1. <a id="shelters-item-1"></a>**Bangladesh safe homes.** The Wage Earners' Welfare Board (WEWB) lists a safe house in Oman <sup>[140](#o140)</sup>. An ILO study published in 2021 found the Embassy running two safe homes in Muscat with places for 60 women and paid an Omani law firm to fight cases while women stayed there <sup>[N9 (2021)](#n9)</sup>. Your family can ask for safe-house help free, directly at WEWB or through the district's DEMO (District Employment and Manpower Office); call 16135, or +8809610102030 from abroad <sup>[140](#o140)</sup>.
2. <a id="shelters-item-2"></a>**Oman's government shelter.** The Ministry of Social Development's shelter takes up to 50 women; residents leave only with a chaperone, and someone with an "absconding" charge is admitted only if she complained against the employer first <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>.
3. <a id="shelters-item-3"></a>**Trafficking hotlines.** The ROP's trafficking hotline, MOL's labour hotline and the shelter hotline run 24 hours, with Bengali speakers on request, though observers report unanswered calls <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>.
4. <a id="shelters-item-4"></a>**No NGO shelters.** There are no NGOs in Oman giving migrant women shelter <sup>[N9 (2021)](#n9)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is the Embassy's safe home in Muscat still open, where is it, and how does a woman get in?
> - What are the ROP trafficking hotline and MOSD shelter hotline numbers?

<a id="changing-employer-or-leaving-1"></a>

### 4.14 Changing employer or leaving

<a id="changing-employer"></a>

#### 4.14.1 Changing employer

The employer pays your dues within 5 days of your last day if you transfer to another employer <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>. From 2027, if you are returned to the office it may transfer you within 15 days without the first employer's consent, once it has refunded him; otherwise it must send you home within 15 days at its cost, and pay your wage if it misses that deadline <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>. If your employer dies, you may stay with the family by agreement for up to 90 days while your status is settled <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a domestic worker change employer without the employer's consent under MD 574/2025?

<a id="ending-the-contract-and-going-home"></a>

#### 4.14.2 Ending the contract and going home

- Either side wanting to end the contract at its term gives 2 months' notice; otherwise it renews <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.
- The employer sends you home within 30 days of the contract ending unless you transfer, gives you a clearance letter on request, and pays your dues at least 3 days before you fly <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.
- The employer pays your ticket if he or his family abused you; you pay it if you quit without a legitimate reason or are dismissed for misconduct <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.
- The employer can dismiss you without end-of-service pay for assault, over 3 days' unexcused absence in a row or 7 in a year, a serious breach or a conviction <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.
- You cannot leave Oman with an "absconding" charge, overstay fines or a sponsorship not cancelled <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>.

One woman sent home in 2021 had become pregnant during abuse, was handed to the police and gave birth in a deportation camp <sup>[N61 (2021)](#n61)</sup>.

<a id="visit-visa-tourist-family-and-friends"></a>

## 5 Visit visa (tourist, family and friends)

ROP issues every visit visa through its e-visa portal, evisa.rop.gov.om, where four kinds of visa are on offer: the unsponsored tourist visa, the sponsored tourist visa, the tourist visa for GCC residents and the express visa <sup>[36](#o36), [88](#o88)</sup>. "Unsponsored" means the visitor applies alone; "sponsored" means a hotel, a licensed tour company or a relative resident in Oman applies for him and is responsible for him <sup>[82](#o82), [141](#o141), [43](#o43)</sup>. A Bangladeshi visitor cannot work on any of them (see [Can you work?](#can-you-work)) <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

What a Bangladeshi can get now depends on where he lives:

- **Living in a GCC country (Bahrain, Kuwait, Qatar, Saudi Arabia or the UAE):** the 29A GCC Resident Visa, if his job is on ROP's approved list, or the 29B or 28B companion visas (Route A) <sup>[142](#o142), [N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>.
- **Living in Bangladesh, visiting a relative in Oman:** the family visit visa, sponsored by the relative, one of the categories exempted in June 2024 (Route B) <sup>[43](#o43), [N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>.
- **Living in Bangladesh, as a tourist:** only as a "high-income tourist" under the June 2024 exemption, through a sponsored tourist visa from a licensed Omani tour company or through the Oman Embassy in Dhaka (Route C) <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3), [141](#o141)</sup>.

The unsponsored tourist visa that most nationalities use is not open to Bangladeshis (see [Visas not open to Bangladeshis](#visas-not-open-to-bangladeshis)) <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>.

<a id="how-the-e-visa-portal-works-all-routes"></a>

### 5.1 How the e-visa portal works (all routes)

- **Language:** Arabic and English only <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
- **Who may fill it in:** anybody can fill in and submit an e-visa application for someone else, and a person without a computer can get help from someone who knows the internet or from a tourist travel office <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
- **Account:** you must log in to start an application; the "Tourist" login is for visitors, and the "PRO / Sponsor" login is only for residents of Oman with a valid civil number (the number on the Omani resident card) <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>.
- **Uploads:** the passport scan and the photo must each be no more than 512 KB <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
- **Timing:** ROP recommends applying at least 4 days before you travel; processing time varies <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
- **Payment:** online; a copy of the application and the payment receipt is emailed to you, and paying does not guarantee a visa <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
- **If ROP asks for more information:** log in, open Visa Application Inquiry, choose the request, add the data and a comment, and resubmit <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
- **Reprinting an approved visa:** log in, search for the application by at least the travel document (passport) number, click "view visa details" and then "generate PDF" <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
- **Help:** the e-visa call centre, 80080011 (toll-free inside Oman) or +968 22848200 from abroad, Info-OmaneVisa@rop.gov.om, 07:00 to 21:00 Oman time <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>.

<a id="route-a-bangladeshis-living-in-a-gcc-country-29a-29b-28b"></a>

### 5.2 Route A: Bangladeshis living in a GCC country (29A, 29B, 28B)

<a id="step-1-check-you-qualify"></a>

#### 5.2.1 Step 1: Check you qualify

You'll need all of these for the 29A GCC Resident Visa <sup>[143](#o143), [88](#o88)</sup>:

- a GCC residence permit with at least 6 months left on the day you enter Oman;
- a job title on ROP's approved professions list;
- a passport valid at least 6 months on entry;
- no other current Oman visa;
- to be outside Oman when you apply.

The approved list covers professional and skilled jobs, among them doctor, nurse, engineer, accountant, teacher, businessman, investor, sales executive, supervisor, "employee", mechanic, electrician, generator operator and nurse assistant <sup>[144](#o144)</sup>. It does not list labourer, driver, cleaner or housemaid <sup>[144](#o144)</sup>. Open the list on evisa.rop.gov.om/visa-eligibility: choose Bangladesh, answer "Yes" to GCC residence, pick your country, then "Click here to see list of approved professions" <sup>[142](#o142), [144](#o144)</sup>.

If your job is not on the list, you can still go on a **29B** if you travel with a GCC resident whose job is on it and you are that person's family member or domestic worker, or on a **28B** (free) if you live in Bahrain, Qatar or the UAE and travel with a GCC citizen whose family member or domestic worker you are <sup>[145](#o145), [146](#o146)</sup>. A female domestic worker can enter on either only if her employer is travelling with his family <sup>[145](#o145), [146](#o146)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Must the GCC residence have 6 months left on entry <sup>[143](#o143)</sup> or only 3 months <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>?
> - Does ROP check the job title printed on the GCC residence card, or the one typed into the application? <sup>[143](#o143)</sup>

<a id="step-2-apply-on-evisaropgovom"></a>

#### 5.2.2 Step 2: Apply on evisa.rop.gov.om

1. <a id="step-2-apply-on-evisaropgovom-item-1"></a>**Register and log in** as a Tourist user <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-apply-on-evisaropgovom-item-2"></a>**Choose the visa:** 29A (main traveller with an approved job) or 29B (companion) <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-2-apply-on-evisaropgovom-item-3"></a>**Upload** a digital photo and a passport copy, each under 512 KB, and a copy of your GCC residence card <sup>[143](#o143), [88](#o88), [147](#o147)</sup>. A 29B applicant also uploads the main traveller's visa <sup>[145](#o145)</sup>. A 28B applicant uploads a photo, passport copy and residence card copy, proof of the family relationship (not needed if the names and passport details show it) and a copy of the GCC citizen's passport or ID card valid more than 6 months <sup>[146](#o146)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-2-apply-on-evisaropgovom-item-4"></a>**Pay RO 5** online for a 29A or 29B; the 28B is free <sup>[143](#o143), [142](#o142)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-2-apply-on-evisaropgovom-item-5"></a>**Wait for the e-visa by email** <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What are the exact registration fields and the payment cards the e-visa portal accepts? <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>

<a id="step-3-check-the-visa-and-travel"></a>

#### 5.2.3 Step 3: Check the visa and travel

1. <a id="step-3-check-the-visa-and-travel-item-1"></a>**Check it is real** on ROP's "Track Your Application" page, evisa.rop.gov.om/en/track-your-application: type the visa application number and your passport number, choose "BANGLADESH" as the document's nationality, and copy the verification text <sup>[148](#o148)</sup>. Or call the e-visa call centre <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-3-check-the-visa-and-travel-item-2"></a>**Enter Oman within one month** of the visa's issue, the limit for visas without a sponsor in Oman; it is single entry <sup>[88](#o88), [143](#o143)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-3-check-the-visa-and-travel-item-3"></a>**At the border:** the 28B can also be applied for on arrival at the Omani border; you must enter with the GCC citizen and directly from a GCC country <sup>[146](#o146)</sup>.

<a id="how-long-you-can-stay"></a>

#### 5.2.4 How long you can stay

- **29A and 29B:** 4 weeks; the applicant can extend once by 1 week himself, and any later extension only in person at what ROP calls a DGPCS office <sup>[143](#o143), [145](#o145)</sup>.
- **28B:** one month, extendable once by one month <sup>[146](#o146)</sup>.
- **Overstay:** RO 10 for each day on any of the three <sup>[143](#o143), [145](#o145), [146](#o146)</sup>.

<a id="route-b-family-visit-visa-a-relative-in-oman-sponsors-you"></a>

### 5.3 Route B: Family visit visa (a relative in Oman sponsors you)

A relative of an Omani citizen, or of a foreigner living in Oman, can be granted a family visit visa at the sponsor's request and on his responsibility <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>. On Oman's government portal gov.om it appears as "Get Family Visa": for relatives such as a foreign employee's parents or siblings, at the authorities' discretion and at the request and on the responsibility of the employer <sup>[149](#o149)</sup>. Family visas are one of the categories exempted from the Bangladesh suspension in June 2024 <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-the-sponsor-checks-he-qualifies"></a>

#### 5.3.1 Step 1: The sponsor checks he qualifies

- His own spouse and children must live in Oman <sup>[149](#o149)</sup>.
- His employer must make or back the request <sup>[149](#o149)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does the gov.om condition that "the sponsored individual's family (spouse and children)" must live in Oman mean the resident sponsor's own family? <sup>[149](#o149)</sup>
> - What minimum salary or job grade must a Bangladeshi resident have to sponsor a family visit? <sup>[149](#o149)</sup>
> - Can a resident sponsor a sibling or a friend, or only parents? <sup>[43](#o43), [149](#o149)</sup>

<a id="step-2-gather-the-papers"></a>

#### 5.3.2 Step 2: Gather the papers

You'll need <sup>[149](#o149)</sup>:

- a personal photo of the visitor;
- a copy of the visitor's passport (valid at least 6 months on entry) <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>;
- a copy of the sponsor's passport and resident card;
- proof of the family relationship, such as birth or marriage certificates;
- a copy of the sponsor's employment contract;
- an application letter.

Papers for the exempted categories must be duly attested to prove eligibility <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>. Since 16 February 2026 Bangladeshi documents can be attested by Oman electronically, without visiting the Omani mission, at www.omanpost.om/attestation-services <sup>[73](#o73)</sup>. For attestation in Bangladesh first, see [Family joining visa](#family-joining-visa-spouse-and-children), [Step 2: Apply on evisa.rop.gov.om](#step-2-apply-on-evisaropgovom).

<a id="step-3-the-sponsor-applies-and-pays"></a>

#### 5.3.3 Step 3: The sponsor applies and pays

1. <a id="step-3-the-sponsor-applies-and-pays-item-1"></a>**Apply online:** since April 2025 a resident can apply for sponsored visas himself on the e-visa portal using electronic authentication: he activates it with the chip (PKI key) in his Omani ID card and sets a PIN, then fills in the forms, uploads the documents, pays and tracks the application, without visiting an ROP service office <sup>[87](#o87)</sup>. Companies apply through their PRO (public relations officer) after registering with the Directorate General of Passports and Residence <sup>[87](#o87)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-3-the-sponsor-applies-and-pays-item-2"></a>**Or through the Oman Embassy in Dhaka:** under the June 2024 note, the embassy receives and processes applications in the exempted categories and coordinates with ROP <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3), [N12 (2024)](#n12)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-3-the-sponsor-applies-and-pays-item-3"></a>**Pay RO 30** <sup>[149](#o149)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-3-the-sponsor-applies-and-pays-item-4"></a>**Wait one to four weeks,** the processing time the embassy gave for exempted categories <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does ROP now accept a Bangladeshi family visit application online from the sponsor, or only through the Oman Embassy in Dhaka? <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3), [87](#o87)</sup>
> - What are the embassy's visa hours, fee and submission counter, and does VFS Global now take these applications? <sup>[150](#o150), [44](#o44)</sup>

<a id="step-4-check-and-travel"></a>

#### 5.3.4 Step 4: Check and travel

1. <a id="step-4-check-and-travel-item-1"></a>**Check the visa** on ROP's Track Your Application page (Route A, [Step 3: Check the visa and travel](#step-3-check-the-visa-and-travel)) <sup>[148](#o148)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Within how long of issue must a family visit visa be used? (the e-visa FAQ gives three months only for the sponsored tourist visa) <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>

<a id="how-long-you-can-stay-1"></a>

#### 5.3.5 How long you can stay

Three months, renewable for up to one more month <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>. Extending a visit visa costs RO 20 and needs a passport valid at least 6 months <sup>[151](#o151)</sup>. A stay of more than three months needs a residence visa (see [Family joining visa](#family-joining-visa-spouse-and-children)) <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>.

<a id="route-c-sponsored-tourist-visa-tour-company-for-high-income-tourists"></a>

### 5.4 Route C: Sponsored tourist visa (tour company) for "high-income tourists"

A tour company licensed for tourism activities can sponsor a 30-day tourist visa, extendable for a similar period, for any nationality; it costs RO 5 for 10 days or RO 20 for 30 days <sup>[141](#o141)</sup>. Nationals of countries not on ROP's List 1, such as Bangladesh, must give extra documents <sup>[141](#o141), [37](#o37)</sup>. A sponsored tourist visa must be used within three months of issue <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>. The June 2024 exemption allows visas for "high-income tourists" only <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>. In May 2026 Oman's ambassador in Dhaka met VFS Global (a private visa-application company) to organise the processing of visa-exempt applications for business and tourism <sup>[150](#o150)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What counts as a "high-income tourist" (bank balance, income, travel history)? <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>
> - Which extra documents does ROP require from nationals outside List 1? <sup>[141](#o141)</sup>
> - Are Omani tour companies currently getting sponsored tourist visas approved for Bangladeshis, and at what price? <sup>[141](#o141), [35](#o35)</sup>

<a id="at-muscat-airport"></a>

### 5.5 At Muscat airport

- **Passport:** valid at least 6 months on entry; by law an entry visa must expire at least 30 days before the passport does <sup>[88](#o88), [N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>.
- **The officer decides:** a valid visa does not guarantee entry; entry is at the discretion of the ROP officer at the port of entry <sup>[143](#o143)</sup>.
- **Where you stay is reported:** hotels, and any place housing a foreigner, must report their foreign guests to the police every day <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What do ROP officers at Muscat airport ask a Bangladeshi visa holder to show (return ticket, hotel booking, cash, sponsor's details)? <sup>[143](#o143), [36](#o36)</sup>
> - Does Bangladesh immigration at Dhaka airport stop first-time travellers on Oman visit visas? <sup>[N16 (2023)](#n16)</sup>

<a id="insurance"></a>

### 5.6 Insurance

Health insurance is required of visa-free visitors <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does ROP require health insurance for a Bangladeshi on a family or tourist visit visa, and which insurers are accepted? <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>

<a id="can-you-work"></a>

### 5.7 Can you work?

No. A non-Omani may not start any work in Oman before getting a work permit, and the permit requires legal entry and a contract with a licensed employer <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. A foreigner who works without a permit, or for anyone other than the employer authorised to employ him, faces 10 days to a month in prison and/or a fine of RO 1,000 to 2,000, cancellation of any permit, deportation and a ban from entering Oman <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Since 31 October 2023 a tourist or visit visa can't be converted into a work visa inside Oman, for any nationality (see [Visas not open to Bangladeshis](#visas-not-open-to-bangladeshis)) <sup>[N2 (2023)](#n2), [N16 (2023)](#n16)</sup>. A sponsor who cannot produce his visitor, or say where he is, within a month of being asked is fined up to RO 500 <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>.

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused"></a>

### 5.8 If the visa is refused

Paying the fee does not guarantee a visa <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>. If ROP asks for more information instead of refusing, add it and resubmit ([How the e-visa portal works](#how-the-e-visa-portal-works-all-routes)) <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does ROP give a reason for refusing a visit visa, and is there any appeal or wait before reapplying? <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>
> - Is the fee refunded after a refusal? (the e-visa FAQ sends payment issues to the call centre) <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>

<a id="scams-2"></a>

### 5.9 Scams

<a id="a-visit-visa-sold-as-a-job"></a>

#### 5.9.1 A visit visa sold as a job

Bangladeshis seldom go to Oman as tourists, but many have gone on visit visas, and many then found no work; how such routes end, in joblessness and trafficking towards Europe, is under What goes wrong most <sup>[N16 (2023)](#n16)</sup>.

**How to spot it:** a "visit visa" or "family visa" that comes with a job, a promise to "convert" it after arrival, or a broker's fee of lakhs of taka. A visitor may not work, conversion has been stopped since 2023, and the visit visa itself costs RO 5 to RO 30 <sup>[8](#o8), [N16 (2023)](#n16), [143](#o143), [141](#o141), [149](#o149)</sup>. Any job in Oman needs a work permit first (see [Background](#background)) <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

<a id="fake-e-visas-and-fake-documents"></a>

#### 5.9.2 Fake e-visas and fake documents

Check any visa on ROP's Track Your Application page (Route A, [Step 3: Check the visa and travel](#step-3-check-the-visa-and-travel)); a visa that doesn't show there isn't real <sup>[148](#o148)</sup>. The only official portal is evisa.rop.gov.om, and its fees are RO 5 to RO 30 for visit visas <sup>[36](#o36), [143](#o143), [141](#o141), [149](#o149)</sup>.

**Tried by migrants:** giving false information or untrue documents (a fake bank statement, a fake relationship certificate, a fake hotel booking) to get any Oman visa is punished by 1 month to 3 years in prison and/or a fine of RO 100 to 500 <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>. A foreigner deported from Oman can't get an entry visa for two years <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>.

<a id="where-to-get-help"></a>

### 5.10 Where to get help

- **Visa questions and payment problems:** ROP e-visa call centre, 80080011 (toll-free in Oman) or +968 22848200, Info-OmaneVisa@rop.gov.om, 07:00 to 21:00 Oman time <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>.
- **Exempted-category applications from Bangladesh:** Embassy of the Sultanate of Oman, Plot No. 1, Road No. 68, Dhaka; dhaka@fm.gov.om; +880 2-984 0398 or +880 2-984 0332 <sup>[44](#o44)</sup>.
- **In trouble in Oman:** Embassy of Bangladesh, Way 3052, Building 4207, Shatti Al Qurum, Muscat; mission.muscat@mofa.gov.bd; +968 24698336, 24698098, 24634798 or 2469 8789 <sup>[152](#o152)</sup>.

<a id="business-visit"></a>

## 6 Business visit

Oman has several visas for business visitors, all requested by a sponsor in Oman: the Business Visit Visa (type 5) and the Business Man Visa (type 10) for a professional or business person, the sponsored multiple-entry visit visa (type 11) for certain occupations, and the Express Visa (type 8) <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>. Investors are one of the categories exempted from the Bangladesh suspension in June 2024, and the exempted professions include doctors, engineers, nurses, teachers and accountants <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>. In May 2026 Oman's embassy in Dhaka discussed with VFS Global how to organise visa-exempt applications for business and tourism <sup>[150](#o150)</sup>.

<a id="express-visa"></a>

### 6.1 Express visa

For businessmen, or holders of rare, high-level professions, at a local sponsor's request <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>. It can also be issued through Oman's embassies abroad <sup>[153](#o153)</sup>.

1. <a id="express-visa-item-1"></a>**The Omani company applies** on the e-visa portal through its PRO, with your passport copy, your photo and its request letter <sup>[153](#o153), [87](#o87)</sup>.
2. <a id="express-visa-item-2"></a>**You must be outside Oman,** hold no other valid Oman visa and have a passport valid at least 6 months <sup>[153](#o153)</sup>.
3. <a id="express-visa-item-3"></a>**The fee is RO 30** <sup>[153](#o153)</sup>.
4. <a id="express-visa-item-4"></a>**Check the visa** on ROP's Track Your Application page (Visit visa, Route A, [Step 3: Find a real job and check the papers](#step-3-find-a-real-job-and-check-the-papers)) <sup>[148](#o148)</sup>.
5. <a id="express-visa-item-5"></a>**Use it within one month** of issue; it is single entry <sup>[88](#o88), [43](#o43)</sup>.

**Stay:** up to 3 weeks, extendable by 1 week <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>.

<a id="multiple-entry-visit-visa"></a>

### 6.2 Multiple-entry visit visa

Costs RO 50; you must be outside Oman, hold no other valid visa and have a passport valid more than 6 months; it allows a stay of up to 3 months each time <sup>[154](#o154)</sup>. The sponsor sends a request letter with your passport copy and photo <sup>[154](#o154)</sup>.

<a id="official-visits-and-events"></a>

### 6.3 Official visits and events

- **Official Visit Visa (type 7):** at the request of the government body you are visiting; three months, renewable for the same period <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>.
- **Festivals Visa (type 22):** to take part in a festival or exhibition approved by the Ministry of Commerce and Industry <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>.

**Can you work?** No: business visitors are bound by the work-permit rule under Visit visa, Can you work? <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does ROP issue the express, business visit or multiple-entry visit visa to Bangladeshis now, through a sponsor's e-visa account or only through the Oman Embassy in Dhaka? <sup>[43](#o43), [N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>
> - What do the Business Visit Visa (type 5) and Business Man Visa (type 10) cost, and how long do they allow? <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>

<a id="scams-3"></a>

### 6.4 Scams

A business or express visa sold with a job works like the visit visa sold as a job (see Visit visa, Scams): no visitor may work in Oman without a work permit, and since 31 October 2023 no visit visa can be converted into a work visa inside Oman <sup>[8](#o8), [N2 (2023)](#n2)</sup>. Giving false papers, such as a fake company invitation, to get any Oman visa is punished by 1 month to 3 years in prison and/or a fine of RO 100 to 500 <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What business-visa scams are reported for Bangladeshis going to Oman, and what do brokers charge for a "business visa"? <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>

<a id="where-to-get-help-1"></a>

### 6.5 Where to get help

- **Visa questions:** ROP e-visa call centre, 80080011 (toll-free in Oman) or +968 22848200, Info-OmaneVisa@rop.gov.om, 07:00 to 21:00 Oman time <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>.
- **Applying from Bangladesh in an exempted category (investor or professional):** Embassy of the Sultanate of Oman, Plot No. 1, Road No. 68, Dhaka; dhaka@fm.gov.om; +880 2-984 0398 or +880 2-984 0332 <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3), [44](#o44)</sup>.
- **In trouble in Oman:** Embassy of Bangladesh in Muscat; mission.muscat@mofa.gov.bd; +968 24698336 <sup>[152](#o152)</sup>.

<a id="student-visa"></a>

## 7 Student visa

An Oman student visa is requested by a licensed educational or training institution, on its responsibility, for a foreigner coming to study; a student cannot apply alone <sup>[55](#o55), [155](#o155), [156](#o156)</sup>. Oman's Ministry of Higher Education, Research and Innovation (MoHERI) licenses private universities and colleges and handles complaints against them; 12 of the 16 private universities and colleges are in Muscat <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>. Students are not among the categories exempted from the Bangladesh suspension in June 2024 <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Are student visas being issued to Bangladeshis under the suspension? <sup>[N2 (2023)](#n2), [N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>

<a id="step-1-choose-an-institution-licensed-to-take-foreign-students"></a>

### 7.1 Step 1: Choose an institution licensed to take foreign students

The institution needs the authorities' approval to host students from abroad <sup>[155](#o155)</sup>. Universities that say they arrange student visas include Middle East College and the National University of Science and Technology in Muscat, and Muscat College <sup>[N63](#n63), [N64 (2024)](#n64), [N65 (2021)](#n65)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Where does MoHERI publish its current list of licensed institutions approved to host foreign students? <sup>[155](#o155), [157](#o157)</sup>

<a id="step-2-pay-the-institution-and-give-it-your-papers"></a>

### 7.2 Step 2: Pay the institution and give it your papers

Examples of what institutions ask:

- **Middle East College:** passport valid 6 months, a medical report approved by Oman's Ministry of Health, two photos, one year of medical insurance issued in Oman, an advance registration fee of RO 1,000 (later counted against tuition), a refundable security deposit of RO 250 and a visa processing fee of RO 35 <sup>[N63](#n63)</sup>.
- **Muscat College:** 8 photos (4 x 6 cm), passport copy, the guardians' passports, a no-objection letter from the guardian, a medical fitness certificate endorsed by Oman's Ministry of Health (required of Bangladeshis), a registration letter and a refundable RO 500 payment <sup>[N65 (2021)](#n65)</sup>.

ROP's own list adds two 6 x 4 cm photos, a medical certificate for nationals of Bangladesh and ten other countries, and copies of the institution's approval to host foreign students and its commercial registration <sup>[155](#o155)</sup>.

<a id="step-3-the-institution-applies-to-rop"></a>

### 7.3 Step 3: The institution applies to ROP

The fee is RO 30 <sup>[156](#o156)</sup>. Middle East College says the visa takes 10 to 15 working days after all documents are in <sup>[N63](#n63)</sup>. The decision is ROP's, and a university is not responsible for a refusal <sup>[N64 (2024)](#n64)</sup>.

<a id="step-4-check-and-travel-1"></a>

### 7.4 Step 4: Check and travel

Check the visa on ROP's Track Your Application page (Visit visa, Route A, [Step 3: The institution applies to ROP](#step-3-the-institution-applies-to-rop)) <sup>[148](#o148)</sup>. Use it within 6 months of issue <sup>[155](#o155)</sup>.
