<a id="how-long-you-can-stay-2"></a>

### 7.5 How long you can stay

The visa allows a stay of 1 to 2 years from entry, multiple entry, renewable while you keep studying with a letter from the institution <sup>[155](#o155)</sup>. Middle East College renews yearly; National University issues 2 years at a time <sup>[N63](#n63), [N64 (2024)](#n64)</sup>.

<a id="family"></a>

### 7.6 Family

Muscat College says a student can apply for a spouse and children under 21 to join; National University normally sponsors no family, and its students can't sponsor parents <sup>[N65 (2021)](#n65), [N64 (2024)](#n64)</sup>.

<a id="can-you-work-1"></a>

### 7.7 Can you work?

No. National University says students may not work full-time or part-time on a student visa, except for a study-related internship with its no-objection letter <sup>[N64 (2024)](#n64)</sup>. Working without a permit carries the penalties under Visit visa, Can you work? <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

<a id="scams-4"></a>

### 7.8 Scams

Check that the institution is licensed by MoHERI, which handles complaints against private institutions <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>. A fee to an agent for a "student visa with a job" is a warning sign: students can't work <sup>[N64 (2024)](#n64)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What student-visa scams are reported for Bangladeshis going to Oman? <sup>[N64 (2024)](#n64)</sup>
> - Is there an appeal if a student visa is refused? <sup>[N64 (2024)](#n64)</sup>

<a id="where-to-get-help-2"></a>

### 7.9 Where to get help

- **A complaint against a private university or college:** MoHERI, which handles complaints against private higher education institutions <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>.
- **Visa status questions:** ROP e-visa call centre, 80080011 (toll-free in Oman) or +968 22848200, Info-OmaneVisa@rop.gov.om <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>.
- **In trouble in Oman:** Embassy of Bangladesh in Muscat; mission.muscat@mofa.gov.bd; +968 24698336 <sup>[152](#o152)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which MoHERI office, phone or online form takes a foreign student's complaint against a private college? <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>

<a id="transit"></a>

## 8 Transit

Transfer passengers at Muscat International Airport stay in the transfer area: they pass a security screening and go to the departure gate, with transfer counters for missed connections <sup>[158](#o158)</sup>. Check at check-in that your bags are checked through to your final destination <sup>[158](#o158)</sup>.

Muscat airport says a transit passenger may enter Oman if the stopover is at least 6 hours, and advises calling ROP first on +968 2451 8746 to check the requirements <sup>[159](#o159)</sup>. Two transit visas exist:

- **Airport Transit Visa (type 23):** requested by your airline for passengers passing through Oman; up to 72 hours, single entry, RO 5; you need enough money and an onward ticket, and a passport valid at least 6 months <sup>[38](#o38), [160](#o160)</sup>.
- **Short Transit Visa (type 27):** to cross Oman by land to a neighbouring country within five days, for nationals of, or holders of a valid visa for, that country <sup>[38](#o38)</sup>.

Ship crews joining or leaving a ship through an Omani airport get a Sailor's Transit Visa, requested by the ship's agent, for up to seven days <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>.

**Can you work?** No: a transit visa gives no right to work, and any work needs a work permit first (see Visit visa, Can you work?) <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

**Where to get help:**
- **Entry during a stopover:** ROP, +968 2451 8746 <sup>[159](#o159)</sup>.
- **Visa questions:** ROP e-visa call centre, 80080011 (toll-free in Oman) or +968 22848200, Info-OmaneVisa@rop.gov.om <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>.
- **Lost items at Muscat airport:** ROP office on the ground floor, +968 2435 5880 <sup>[159](#o159)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Do airlines (Oman Air, SalamAir, Biman) get airport transit visas for Bangladeshi passengers under the suspension? <sup>[160](#o160), [N2 (2023)](#n2)</sup>
> - Can a Bangladeshi transit airside at Muscat without any visa? <sup>[158](#o158)</sup>
> - Do brokers sell Muscat transit or "stopover" visas to Bangladeshis as a way into Oman or onward to Europe, and what happens to those caught? <sup>[N16 (2023)](#n16), [N18 (2023)](#n18)</sup>

<a id="medical-treatment-visit"></a>

## 9 Medical treatment visit

Oman has a Patient Visa (type 35) for a short visit by someone with a medical problem; it is listed among the unsponsored visas, and ROP's e-visa eligibility check offers a Bangladeshi living outside the GCC no unsponsored visa except the 33A Property Owner Visa <sup>[38](#o38), [35](#o35)</sup>. A sponsored Emergency Visa (type 30) allows a short visit in an emergency <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>. Patients are not among the categories exempted from the Bangladesh suspension in June 2024 <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>.

**Can you work?** No: any work needs a work permit first (see Visit visa, Can you work?) <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

**Where to get help:**
- **Visa questions:** ROP e-visa call centre, 80080011 (toll-free in Oman) or +968 22848200, Info-OmaneVisa@rop.gov.om, 07:00 to 21:00 Oman time <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>.
- **Applying from Bangladesh:** Embassy of the Sultanate of Oman, Plot No. 1, Road No. 68, Dhaka; dhaka@fm.gov.om; +880 2-984 0398 or +880 2-984 0332 <sup>[44](#o44)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can an Omani hospital sponsor a Bangladeshi patient, and on which visa? <sup>[38](#o38), [43](#o43)</sup>
> - What do the Patient and Emergency visas cost and allow? <sup>[38](#o38), [43](#o43)</sup>
> - What are the steps and papers for a Bangladeshi patient (hospital letter, medical reports, proof of funds), and how long does it take? <sup>[38](#o38)</sup>
> - Does the Oman Embassy in Dhaka accept Emergency Visa requests, for example for a relative of a worker who is critically ill or has died in Oman? <sup>[43](#o43), [N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>
> - Are agents in Bangladesh selling "medical visas" to Oman as a way to work there? <sup>[38](#o38)</sup>

<a id="family-joining-visa-spouse-and-children"></a>

## 10 Family joining visa (spouse and children)

A foreign worker living in Oman can bring a husband or wife and children under 21 on the Royal Oman Police (ROP) "joining relatives" (family joining) visa; the family member then lives in Oman on a residence that depends on the worker's own valid residence <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>. The employer asks for the visa with a letter taking responsibility for the family member <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.

**Is it open to Bangladeshis?** Yes. When Oman stopped issuing new visas to Bangladeshis on 31 October 2023, family visas were among the categories it exempted in June 2024, and a July 2026 Bangladeshi guide still lists family joining visas for Bangladeshis living in Oman as open <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3), [N19 (2024)](#n19), [N66](#n66)</sup>. Applications from Bangladesh are handled with the Oman Embassy in Dhaka in coordination with ROP, need fully attested documents, and take one to four weeks <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does ROP still route a Bangladeshi family-joining application through the Oman Embassy in Dhaka in 2026, or does the employer apply in Oman like for other nationalities? <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>

<a id="who-can-apply"></a>

### 10.1 Who can apply

<a id="the-worker-sponsor"></a>

#### 10.1.1 The worker (sponsor)

ROP's English family-joining page and its Arabic family-visa page say the worker must hold a senior job by the unified Gulf (GCC) job classification, rent housing in his own or his employer's name, and earn at least RO 600 a month <sup>[122](#o122), [161](#o161)</sup>. An ROP official said in February 2023 that the threshold had been cut from RO 350 to RO 150, and that the employer can also decide whether the worker may have his family with him <sup>[N55 (2023)](#n55)</sup>. ROP's service page on Gov.om (the government services portal), updated May 2026, and ROP's Arabic family-joining page list no salary threshold; Gov.om asks only for a valid passport, the worker's valid residence and a letter from the employer taking responsibility <sup>[121](#o121), [162](#o162)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What monthly salary does ROP actually apply in 2026 when a Bangladeshi worker sponsors his family: RO 150, RO 350, RO 600 or none? <sup>[122](#o122), [161](#o161), [162](#o162), [N55 (2023)](#n55)</sup>
> - Can a worker in a labour-category job (cleaner, labourer, driver) sponsor family if his salary meets the threshold? <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>
> - Does ROP check the salary through the Ministry of Labour (MoL) contract record or the bank statements? <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>
> - Can a domestic worker, who is employed by a household, sponsor a spouse or child at all? <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>

<a id="who-can-come"></a>

#### 10.1.2 Who can come

- the spouse <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>;
- children under 21: a child's residence ends at 21 unless the child gets a residence of its own, and the Director General of Passports and Residence can waive the age limit for humanitarian reasons <sup>[89](#o89), [N67 (2019)](#n67)</sup>;
- the authority decides case by case for children over 21 <sup>[N67 (2019)](#n67)</sup>.

<a id="parents"></a>

#### 10.1.3 Parents

The family joining visa does not cover parents <sup>[121](#o121), [55](#o55)</sup>. ROP can, at its discretion, give a separate family visa (RO 30) for a worker's parents or siblings, on the employer's request and responsibility, if the worker's own spouse and children live in Oman <sup>[149](#o149), [55](#o55)</sup>. The employer applies with the relative's photo and passport copy, the worker's passport and resident card copies, proof of relationship, the employment contract and an application letter <sup>[149](#o149)</sup>. Parents can also come on the visit visa for relatives, requested by the employer on its responsibility: up to three months from entry plus one month's extension, single entry, RO 20 <sup>[163](#o163)</sup>. An investor, a property owner or a Golden Residency holder can bring parents on joining residence (see [Investor and long-term residence](#investor-and-long-term-residence)) <sup>[55](#o55), [N68](#n68)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is the family visa for a worker's parents followed by a residence card, and for how long, or is it only a visit? <sup>[149](#o149), [55](#o55)</sup>

<a id="a-foreign-wife-of-an-omani"></a>

#### 10.1.4 A foreign wife of an Omani

She gets a joining visa at her husband's request on a certificate from the Ministry of Interior proving the marriage <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>.

<a id="cost-and-time"></a>

### 10.2 Cost and time

- Joining relatives visa: RO 30; valid two years from stamping, multiple entry <sup>[121](#o121), [122](#o122)</sup>
- Medical fitness exam in Oman for the resident card: RO 30, for each family member aged 16 and over <sup>[50](#o50)</sup>
- Resident card: RO 5 for each year of validity <sup>[N32](#n32)</sup>
- Attesting the marriage certificate at Oman's Foreign Ministry: RO 10 <sup>[74](#o74)</sup>
- Processing: one to four weeks for Bangladeshi applications <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>

The visa must be used to enter Oman within three months of issue <sup>[N67 (2019)](#n67)</sup>. Late renewal or late registration of the residence costs RO 50 <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What does a Sanad office (licensed government-services typing centre) charge to type and submit a family joining application? <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>

<a id="step-1-gather-the-papers"></a>

### 10.3 Step 1: Gather the papers

You'll need <sup>[121](#o121), [161](#o161), [122](#o122)</sup>:
- a copy of your passport and your resident card;
- each family member's passport copy, valid at least six months, and digital photos;
- proof of relationship: the attested marriage certificate (nikahnama) for a spouse and the attested birth certificate for each child ([Step 2: Attest the marriage and birth certificates](#step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates));
- a letter from your employer taking responsibility for the family member's visa;
- a Ministry of Labour form showing your job title and salary, or six months of bank statements showing your salary transfers;
- a copy of your house lease or another document showing where you live;
- for each adult (15 or over on ROP's Arabic page) coming from Bangladesh, an original medical certificate approved by Oman's Ministry of Health, with a copy <sup>[161](#o161)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which medical check in Bangladesh does ROP accept for a dependant before the visa: a Wafid (GCC-approved centre) report attested on Oman's Ministry of Health portal, or another certificate? <sup>[122](#o122), [161](#o161)</sup>

<a id="step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates"></a>

### 10.4 Step 2: Attest the marriage and birth certificates

ROP wants the marriage certificate authenticated by Oman's Ministry of Foreign Affairs (MoFA) <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates-item-1"></a>**In Bangladesh, notarise the nikahnama and the children's birth certificates, then have them attested by the Law Ministry and then by the Ministry of Foreign Affairs (MOFA) in Dhaka** <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>. For a birth certificate from Cox's Bazar district, the Deputy Commissioner's office attests instead of the Law Ministry <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates-item-2"></a>**Or use an apostille.** Bangladesh joined the Apostille Convention (which replaces the embassy-legalisation chain with one certificate) in force from 30 March 2025, and Oman has been a member since 2012 <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>. Oman is not among the states that objected to Bangladesh joining, and Bangladesh's MOFA lists Oman among the 114 countries that accept Bangladeshi apostilles <sup>[70](#o70), [71](#o71)</sup>. The e-Apostille is applied for on the MyGov platform and takes about five days, but MOFA says acceptance abroad of marriage certificates is still being worked on <sup>[72](#o72)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does ROP accept a Bangladeshi e-Apostille on a nikahnama in place of Oman's MoFA attestation? <sup>[71](#o71), [72](#o72)</sup>

3. <a id="step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates-item-3"></a>**Then get Oman's MoFA attestation through Oman Post.** Since 16 February 2026 Bangladeshi documents can be attested online at omanpost.om/attestation-services without visiting Oman's Embassy in Dhaka <sup>[73](#o73)</sup>. MoFA no longer attests at its own counters: in Oman, use the same website or an Oman Post branch (Muscat Airport Heights or Qurum, Sur, Sohar, Nizwa, Salalah, Buraimi, Khasab or Madha) <sup>[74](#o74)</sup>. A marriage contract costs RO 10 <sup>[74](#o74)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What does Oman Post charge on top of MoFA's RO 10, and how long does e-attestation of a nikahnama take? <sup>[74](#o74), [73](#o73)</sup>
   > - What does MoFA charge to attest a birth certificate, which is not on its fee list? <sup>[74](#o74)</sup>

<a id="step-3-the-employer-applies"></a>

### 10.5 Step 3: The employer applies

1. <a id="step-3-the-employer-applies-item-1"></a>**The employer or its authorised signatory submits ROP's electronic form,** typed in English for non-Arabs, with the papers from [Step 1: Gather the papers](#step-1-gather-the-papers) and the employer's authorised-signatory specimen <sup>[122](#o122), [161](#o161)</sup>. Gov.om lists the steps as submission, examination and issue <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-3-the-employer-applies-item-2"></a>**Pay the RO 30 visa fee** <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-3-the-employer-applies-item-3"></a>**Wait.** ROP may refuse any application without giving a reason <sup>[89](#o89)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is there any appeal against a refused family joining visa? <sup>[89](#o89)</sup>

<a id="step-4-check-the-visa-and-travel"></a>

### 10.6 Step 4: Check the visa and travel

1. <a id="step-4-check-the-visa-and-travel-item-1"></a>**Check the visa is real** with ROP's eVisa call centre: 80080011 inside Oman or +968 22848200 from abroad, or Info-omanevisa@rop.gov.om, 7 am to 9 pm <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-4-check-the-visa-and-travel-item-2"></a>**Enter within three months of issue** <sup>[N67 (2019)](#n67)</sup>. A valid visa does not guarantee entry: the passport officer can refuse entry on public-interest grounds <sup>[89](#o89)</sup>.

<a id="step-5-after-you-arrive"></a>

### 10.7 Step 5: After you arrive

1. <a id="step-5-after-you-arrive-item-1"></a>**Medical fitness exam for adults.** For each family member aged 16 or over, buy the medical form on the Ministry of Health eHealth portal or at a Sanad office (RO 30), then go to a private screening centre with the QR code; the form is valid 30 days <sup>[50](#o50)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-5-after-you-arrive-item-2"></a>**Resident card within 30 days of entry.** Each family member must get a resident card in person at an ROP civil status office within 30 days; a late application is fined <sup>[51](#o51), [122](#o122)</sup>. Bring the passport and the medical form <sup>[51](#o51)</sup>. The card costs RO 5 for each year of validity and can be valid up to 10 years, for categories the Director General sets; renew within 30 days of expiry <sup>[N32](#n32)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-5-after-you-arrive-item-3"></a>**Renew in Oman.** The family member must be in Oman to renew the residence <sup>[89](#o89)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - For how many years does ROP issue a dependant's resident card in 2026: the length of the worker's own residence, or longer? <sup>[N32](#n32)</sup>

<a id="can-a-spouse-work"></a>

### 10.8 Can a spouse work?

Not on the joining residence: ROP allows an employee to work only for his or her own sponsor <sup>[89](#o89)</sup>. The authority can convert a visa into a work visa inside Oman if the conditions are met and the fee is paid (see [Changing visa type](#changing-visa-type), "Family dependant to work") <sup>[N69 (2021)](#n69)</sup>.

<a id="if-you-change-employer-or-lose-the-job"></a>

### 10.9 If you change employer or lose the job

When your residence moves to a new employer, your family's joining residence moves with it if they still meet the conditions; until the move is done, your first employer stays responsible <sup>[N70 (2020)](#n70)</sup>. The family visa needs the worker's own valid residence <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How many days do dependants have to leave or change status after the sponsoring worker's residence is cancelled? <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>

<a id="scams-5"></a>

### 10.10 Scams

- **Fake nikahnamas.** Giving false information or forged documents to register a civil-status event in Oman is a crime punishable by prison, a fine of up to RO 500, or both <sup>[N71 (1999)](#n71)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Are broker rings using fake nikahnamas to bring Bangladeshi women to Oman on family visas and then put them to work as domestic workers, as Facebook pages in Oman allege, and has ROP or the Embassy acted on it?

- **Overcharging.** The government fee for the visa is RO 30 <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - What do Bangladeshi workers in Oman typically pay in total (typing, attestation, agent) for a family visa?

<a id="where-to-get-help-3"></a>

### 10.11 Where to get help

- **The Bangladesh Embassy in Muscat,** Villa 3126 A-B, Way 3040, Shati Al Qurum, Sunday to Thursday 8 am to 4 pm, for attesting documents (RO 3.900 for the first five pages) and advice; book on amardutabashmuscat.org <sup>[164](#o164), [165](#o165)</sup>.
- **The Embassy's Expatriate Service Cell:** WhatsApp +968 91980621 (text or voice message only), consularmuscat@gmail.com; emergency line +968 91997852 <sup>[166](#o166)</sup>.

<a id="investor-and-long-term-residence"></a>

## 11 Investor and long-term residence

Oman has three long-stay routes that do not need an employer: the Golden Residency for investors, the property-owner residence, and the older investor visa <sup>[167](#o167), [N72](#n72), [N69 (2021)](#n69)</sup>. Investors are among the categories exempted from the ban on new visas for Bangladeshis <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>. For a Bangladeshi not living in another Gulf state, ROP's eVisa wizard lists only one visa that needs no sponsor: the 33A property owner visa, for the owner of a housing unit in an approved tourist complex (RO 50, two years) <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>.

<a id="golden-residency-10-years"></a>

### 11.1 Golden Residency (10 years)

<a id="who-qualifies-1"></a>

#### 11.1.1 Who qualifies

The Golden Residency, launched in Salalah on 31 August 2025 by the Ministry of Commerce, Industry and Investment Promotion (MOCIIP), gives 10 years' residence through any one of seven routes, each starting from RO 200,000 <sup>[N68](#n68), [N73](#n73)</sup>:
- a company at least one year old, with the applicant's stake in its assets worth RO 200,000 or more;
- completed property with a title deed in an Integrated Tourism Complex (ITC) <sup>[N68](#n68)</sup>;
- government development bonds;
- listed shares;
- a fixed bank deposit placed for five years <sup>[N68](#n68)</sup>;
- a company employing 50 or more Omanis;
- nomination as partner or senior professional by a company registered under the Foreign Investment Law.

A separate 5-year retiree route is reported for people aged 60 or over with a fixed monthly income of at least RO 4,000, at least two years' past work in Oman and six months of Omani bank statements <sup>[N74](#n74), [N75](#n75)</sup>. Another route is reported for people who have lived in Oman legally and continuously for at least 15 years, with no absence over 90 days in any year <sup>[N75](#n75)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does a 5-year tier (RO 250,000 investment, or the retiree route) still exist alongside the RO 200,000 10-year route launched in August 2025? <sup>[N74](#n74), [N68](#n68)</sup>
> - Does the 15-year long-residence route exist on the official portal, does it need any investment or income, and can a long-serving worker use it? <sup>[N75](#n75)</sup>

Applicants are reported to need to be 21 or over, with a clean record, good health and proof of accommodation in Oman <sup>[N75](#n75)</sup>. The residency covers first-degree family members with no limit on number or age, and the holder may employ up to three domestic workers and issue visit visas for relatives <sup>[N68](#n68)</sup>.

<a id="how-to-apply"></a>

#### 11.1.2 How to apply

1. <a id="how-to-apply-item-1"></a>**Apply on the official portal,** omanresidence.gov.om (applications at portal.omanresidence.gov.om), which is run with the operator Migrate World; upload the documents and track the application there <sup>[167](#o167), [N73](#n73)</sup>.
2. <a id="how-to-apply-item-2"></a>**Pay the application fee,** reported as RO 551 for 10 years and RO 326 for 5 years, plus medical, insurance, attestation and translation costs <sup>[N75](#n75)</sup>.
3. <a id="how-to-apply-item-3"></a>**Wait for review** by Invest Oman and ROP, reported at three to six weeks for a complete file; then give biometrics at ROP and collect the card <sup>[N75](#n75)</sup>.

Golden Residency gives no direct route to citizenship <sup>[N75](#n75)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which phone number or office of Invest Oman or MOCIIP answers Golden Residency questions? <sup>[167](#o167)</sup>

<a id="investor-visa-and-residence-older-route"></a>

### 11.2 Investor visa and residence (older route)

The investor visa is for a foreigner investing in Oman on a certificate from the competent authority, and must be used within one year of issue <sup>[N69 (2021)](#n69)</sup>. Its fees <sup>[N69 (2021)](#n69)</sup>:
- 10-year investor visa RO 500, renewed at RO 500 every three years;
- 5-year investor visa RO 300, renewed at RO 300 every three years;
- spouse and first-degree relatives: RO 100 (10-year) or RO 50 (5-year), renewed at the same rate every three years.

Investor-joining relatives are exempt from the age limit <sup>[N69 (2021)](#n69)</sup>. An investor who wants to live and work in his own company needs an MoL labour permit stamped "investor", the commercial register, Chamber of Commerce membership, and, for a Bangladeshi, a medical certificate attested by the Ministry of Health <sup>[168](#o168)</sup>. A foreigner planning to invest can also get a one-year multiple-entry visa, extendable, allowing stays of up to three months each time, with his spouse and children <sup>[N69 (2021)](#n69)</sup>.

<a id="property-owner-residence"></a>

### 11.3 Property-owner residence

Since June 2026 a foreigner who owns a real-estate unit, registered or still being registered, gets owner residence without a sponsor on a certificate from the competent authority, and the owner visa requires entry within three months of issue <sup>[N72](#n72)</sup>. A buyer of building land or of a unit not yet registered can get a multiple-entry visa without a sponsor, valid 6 to 12 months and extendable, with stays of up to three months each time, for himself, his spouse and first-degree relatives <sup>[N72](#n72)</sup>. The owner's spouse and first-degree relatives, parents included, get a joining visa without a sponsor for RO 50, with the property registry copy and letters from the Ministry of Housing and Urban Planning and the authority over the property's location <sup>[169](#o169), [55](#o55)</sup>. The residence ends, with the family's, when the property is sold or passes to someone else <sup>[N72](#n72)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What does a property have to be worth for owner residence after Decision 87/2026: is there a minimum price outside the Golden Residency? <sup>[N72](#n72)</sup>

<a id="what-is-realistic-for-bangladeshis"></a>

### 11.4 What is realistic for Bangladeshis

Every route above needs at least RO 200,000 invested, RO 4,000 a month in fixed income, or property bought in Oman <sup>[N68](#n68), [N74](#n74), [N72](#n72)</sup>. Legal residence in Oman comes only through work, investment or family <sup>[170](#o170)</sup>.

<a id="scams-6"></a>

### 11.5 Scams

- **Fake "investor visa" agents.** The only official application channel is the Golden Residency portal on the .gov.om domain, run with Migrate World <sup>[167](#o167), [N73](#n73)</sup>. The smallest investment route starts at RO 200,000 <sup>[N68](#n68)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Which fake investor or "business visa" offers for Oman are reported in Bangladesh, and what do they cost?

- **Investor visa used as a work visa.** An investor who works in his own company still needs an MoL labour permit for it <sup>[168](#o168)</sup>.

<a id="where-to-get-help-4"></a>

### 11.6 Where to get help

- **Golden Residency applications:** the official portal, omanresidence.gov.om, where applications are tracked <sup>[167](#o167)</sup>.
- **Visa questions:** ROP e-visa call centre, 80080011 (toll-free in Oman) or +968 22848200, Info-OmaneVisa@rop.gov.om, 07:00 to 21:00 Oman time <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>.
- **Applying from Bangladesh as an investor:** Embassy of the Sultanate of Oman, Plot No. 1, Road No. 68, Dhaka; dhaka@fm.gov.om; +880 2-984 0398 or +880 2-984 0332 <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3), [44](#o44)</sup>.

<a id="changing-visa-type"></a>

## 12 Changing visa type

ROP's Executive Regulation of the Foreigners' Residence Law lets the authority convert any entry visa into any visa followed by residence, including a work visa, if its conditions are met, under its rules and after the fee is paid <sup>[N69 (2021)](#n69)</sup>. Since 31 October 2023, ROP has suspended converting tourist and visit visas to work visas for all nationalities, so a visitor who gets a job must leave Oman, get the work visa and come back <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3), [N76 (2023)](#n76)</sup>. ROP allows a resident to work only for his or her own sponsor <sup>[89](#o89)</sup>.

<a id="visit-visa-to-work"></a>

### 12.1 Visit visa to work

Not allowed inside Oman <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3), [N76 (2023)](#n76)</sup>. Leave, let the employer get the work visa (RO 20, under an MoL work permit whose profession and gender match the visa), and re-enter (see [Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker)) <sup>[171](#o171)</sup>. For a Bangladeshi this works only in a category exempt from the 2023 visa ban, such as doctor, engineer, nurse, teacher or accountant <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>. Overstaying a visit visa costs RO 10 a day <sup>[115](#o115)</sup>. A Bangladeshi guide warns that going on a visit visa to look for work risks fines and a ban <sup>[N66](#n66)</sup>.

<a id="family-dependant-to-work"></a>

### 12.2 Family dependant to work

The authority can convert a family joining visa into a work visa inside Oman under the general conversion rule <sup>[N69 (2021)](#n69)</sup>. The October 2023 suspension named tourist and visit visas <sup>[N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>, but one adviser reported it also covering dependent visitor visas <sup>[N76 (2023)](#n76)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a spouse on joining residence move to a work visa inside Oman in 2026, and what does ROP charge? <sup>[N69 (2021)](#n69), [N3 (2024)](#n3)</sup>
> - Which MoL and ROP e-services are used, and does the new employer need a new labour licence? <sup>[N31](#n31)</sup>

<a id="changing-employer-without-an-noc"></a>

### 12.3 Changing employer without an NOC

Since 1 January 2021 a worker whose contract has ended can move to another licensed employer without the first employer's no-objection certificate (NOC); the steps and fees are under Work visa: company worker, "Changing employer or leaving" <sup>[N70 (2020)](#n70)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - MoL's transfer e-service lists "approval (previous sponsor)" as a step: can a worker whose contract has ended move without that approval, and how? <sup>[33](#o33), [N70 (2020)](#n70)</sup>

<a id="domestic-worker-to-a-company-job"></a>

### 12.4 Domestic worker to a company job

> **Need to know**
> - Can a domestic worker move to a company job inside Oman, or only to another household (see [Work visa: domestic worker](#work-visa-domestic-worker), "Changing employer or leaving")? <sup>[N31](#n31)</sup>

<a id="changing-the-job-title-profession"></a>

### 12.5 Changing the job title (profession)

The profession on a work licence can be changed only from a lower to a higher fee category, with MoL approval and payment of the difference in fees <sup>[N31](#n31)</sup>. The profession on the visa must match the MoL permit <sup>[171](#o171)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a worker's profession be moved to a lower category (for example engineer to labourer), and how is the change then made on the ROP resident card? <sup>[N31](#n31)</sup>

<a id="asylum"></a>

## 13 Asylum

Oman is not a party to the 1951 Refugee Convention or its 1967 Protocol and has no law or regulations for asylum-seekers or refugees <sup>[170](#o170)</sup>. Its only route is political asylum, granted by Sultani order to a foreigner pursued by a foreign government for political reasons that threaten his life or freedom <sup>[N77 (1995)](#n77), [170](#o170)</sup>. The law names no other ground: economic hardship, unpaid wages or a lost job do not qualify <sup>[N77 (1995)](#n77)</sup>.

<a id="what-the-law-says"></a>

### 13.1 What the law says

- A foreigner pursued by a non-Omani authority for political reasons that threaten his life or freedom may ask for political asylum, if the reasons do not conflict with Oman's general policy or Islamic beliefs (Foreigners' Residence Law, Article 24) <sup>[N77 (1995)](#n77)</sup>.
- The asylum lasts only until the danger has passed <sup>[N77 (1995)](#n77)</sup>.
- The minister responsible for foreign affairs organises refugee matters with the competent authorities <sup>[N77 (1995)](#n77)</sup>.
- Asylum is granted by Sultani order, and the refugee gets a special card (Article 25) <sup>[N77 (1995)](#n77)</sup>.
- A refugee may not take part in any political activity (Article 26) <sup>[N77 (1995)](#n77)</sup>.
- Asylum can be withdrawn or restricted at any time and the refugee expelled, but not to a country where his life or freedom is at risk (Article 27) <sup>[N77 (1995)](#n77)</sup>.
- The Basic Statute (Oman's constitution) forbids extraditing political refugees <sup>[N78 (2021)](#n78)</sup>.
- A refugee without a passport can be given a travel pass to leave Oman (Article 21) <sup>[N77 (1995)](#n77)</sup>.

<a id="unhcr"></a>

### 13.2 UNHCR

UNHCR (the UN refugee agency) counts 289 refugees and 414 asylum-seekers in Oman <sup>[170](#o170)</sup>. People registered with UNHCR stay under ordinary immigration law, and legal residence comes only through work, investment or family <sup>[170](#o170)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Where does UNHCR receive asylum-seekers for Oman, and does it have a staffed office in Muscat? <sup>[170](#o170)</sup>
> - Has any Bangladeshi been granted political asylum in Oman? <sup>[170](#o170)</sup>

<a id="having-a-child-in-oman"></a>

## 14 Having a child in Oman

A child born in Oman to Bangladeshi parents is registered with ROP's Directorate General of Civil Status and, for Bangladeshi papers, with the Bangladesh Embassy in Muscat; it then needs a Bangladeshi passport and its own Omani residence (Steps 3 and 4) <sup>[172](#o172), [173](#o173), [166](#o166), [121](#o121)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-register-the-birth-with-rop-and-get-the-omani-birth-certificate"></a>

### 14.1 Step 1: Register the birth with ROP and get the Omani birth certificate

Registration needs the birth notice, the live-birth form <sup>[N71 (1999)](#n71), [174](#o174)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-1-register-the-birth-with-rop-and-get-the-omani-birth-certificate-item-1"></a>**Report the birth within two weeks** to the civil registrar of ROP's Directorate General of Civil Status, on the set form with the live-birth form <sup>[N71 (1999)](#n71), [172](#o172)</sup>. The father reports it if present; then, in order, an adult relative present at the birth, an adult living with the mother, the doctor or midwife, the hospital director, the area's sheikh, and last the mother; no one else may report it <sup>[N71 (1999)](#n71)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-1-register-the-birth-with-rop-and-get-the-omani-birth-certificate-item-2"></a>**Bring** the birth notice, both parents' identification papers and the original marriage contract, and appear in person <sup>[174](#o174), [172](#o172)</sup>. The report gives the baby's name and sex and each parent's name, civil number, nationality, religion, address and job <sup>[N71 (1999)](#n71)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-1-register-the-birth-with-rop-and-get-the-omani-birth-certificate-item-3"></a>**Pay RO 2 for the certificate,** RO 1 for each extra copy <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-1-register-the-birth-with-rop-and-get-the-omani-birth-certificate-item-4"></a>**The registrar records the baby and gives it a civil number** within seven days of the report <sup>[N71 (1999)](#n71)</sup>.

Not reporting a birth is punishable by up to one year in prison, a fine of up to RO 200, or both <sup>[N71 (1999)](#n71)</sup>.

<a id="step-2-register-the-birth-with-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-muscat"></a>

### 14.2 Step 2: Register the birth with the Bangladesh Embassy in Muscat

Registration is free within 45 days of birth and RO 2 after; corrections and copies cost RO 2 each <sup>[165](#o165), [175](#o175)</sup>. Bangladesh requires births to be registered within 45 days <sup>[N79 (2022)](#n79)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-2-register-the-birth-with-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-muscat-item-1"></a>**Apply on bdris.gov.bd** (Bangladesh's online birth registration system), linked from the Embassy's website; choose Bangladesh Embassy and Oman, Muscat as the registration office <sup>[173](#o173), [N79 (2022)](#n79)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-register-the-birth-with-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-muscat-item-2"></a>**Enter the baby's name** in Bangla and in English capital letters, with the parents' birth-registration and NID numbers <sup>[N79 (2022)](#n79)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-2-register-the-birth-with-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-muscat-item-3"></a>**Submit and print the form;** keep the application number to track it <sup>[N79 (2022)](#n79)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-2-register-the-birth-with-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-muscat-item-4"></a>**If the baby is 46 days or older, pay RO 2** to the Embassy account "Birth and Death Registration Fee, Embassy of Bangladesh, Muscat", 0423006444760022, at any Bank Muscat branch or by online transfer; write your name and passport number or OID in the reference, keep the yellow receipt, and avoid ATM or cash-deposit machines <sup>[165](#o165), [81](#o81)</sup>. The Embassy takes no cash <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-2-register-the-birth-with-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-muscat-item-5"></a>**Book a schedule and attend.** Book on amardutabashmuscat.org or the Embassy's online form (forms.gle/CYHtStaaKWC1afwN6) and come on the date given, about 15 days after the bdris application, with the printed form and the parents' passport copies <sup>[81](#o81), [164](#o164), [N79 (2022)](#n79)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the Muscat Embassy's own document list for a birth registration (its process page returns an error)? <sup>[173](#o173)</sup>
> - Must the Omani birth certificate be attested by Oman's MoFA before the Embassy accepts it? <sup>[74](#o74)</sup>

<a id="step-3-get-the-baby-a-bangladeshi-e-passport"></a>

### 14.3 Step 3: Get the baby a Bangladeshi e-passport

At the Embassy, a 48-page 5-year e-passport costs RO 12.700 (RO 19.100 urgent) for workers and students and RO 42.400 (RO 63.600 urgent) for general applicants <sup>[165](#o165), [175](#o175)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which rate does the Embassy charge a baby: the worker rate or the general rate? <sup>[165](#o165)</sup>

1. <a id="step-3-get-the-baby-a-bangladeshi-e-passport-item-1"></a>**Fill in the online application** at epassport.gov.bd; for a child without an NID, give the father's or mother's NID number <sup>[166](#o166), [60](#o60)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-3-get-the-baby-a-bangladeshi-e-passport-item-2"></a>**Book an enrolment appointment** on amardutabashmuscat.org; no application is accepted without one <sup>[166](#o166)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-3-get-the-baby-a-bangladeshi-e-passport-item-3"></a>**Pay online** to the account "Passport Fee, Embassy of Bangladesh, Muscat", 0423006444760038, or at a Bank Muscat branch <sup>[165](#o165)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-3-get-the-baby-a-bangladeshi-e-passport-item-4"></a>**Bring:** the appointment paper, the printed form with barcode, the bank slip, passport and Oman labour card copies, and the birth registration certificate <sup>[166](#o166)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-3-get-the-baby-a-bangladeshi-e-passport-item-5"></a>**The baby does not attend** if under six: bring a recent 3R lab-printed colour photo with a white background, the baby's birth registration certificate and a copy of a parent's passport, NID or birth certificate <sup>[166](#o166)</sup>. Expect a wait of several hours; keep the delivery slip <sup>[166](#o166)</sup>.
6. <a id="step-3-get-the-baby-a-bangladeshi-e-passport-item-6"></a>**Collect it** by Oman Post delivery within 7 working days for RO 1.050, at a booked Embassy slot, or any Saturday 9 am to 3 pm without appointment; track it on epassport.gov.bd <sup>[166](#o166)</sup>.

<a id="step-4-get-the-babys-omani-residence"></a>

### 14.4 Step 4: Get the baby's Omani residence

A child of a resident worker gets joining residence like any child under 21 (see [Family joining visa](#family-joining-visa-spouse-and-children)) <sup>[121](#o121), [89](#o89)</sup>. Children under 16 need no medical form <sup>[50](#o50)</sup>. The resident card costs RO 5 a year <sup>[N32](#n32)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Within how many days of birth must a newborn's residence visa be issued, and what is the late fine? <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>
> - Can a mother on a work visa sponsor her baby, and must her employer agree? <sup>[N67 (2019)](#n67)</sup>

<a id="if-the-parents-are-not-married"></a>

### 14.5 If the parents are not married

Sex between a man and a woman without a marriage contract is a crime in Oman: six months to three years in prison for both, at least two years if either is married <sup>[N51 (2018)](#n51)</sup>. If neither has a spouse or guardian in Oman, the Public Prosecution may prosecute or deport them <sup>[N51 (2018)](#n51)</sup>. The birth registration needs the original marriage contract, and an illegitimate child is registered only under procedures set by regulation in line with Islamic Sharia <sup>[174](#o174), [N71 (1999)](#n71)</sup>. A woman who aborts her own pregnancy faces six months to three years in prison <sup>[N51 (2018)](#n51)</sup>. In Gulf countries, hospitals must admit a woman in labour, but the baby's birth certificate, travel documents and exit clearance are held until the hospital bill is paid or, in some countries, a marriage certificate is shown <sup>[N80 (2020)](#n80)</sup>. Anyone who finds an abandoned newborn must hand it to the police <sup>[N71 (1999)](#n71)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How does an unmarried Bangladeshi mother in Oman get a birth certificate and a travel document for her baby, and does the Embassy issue a travel permit for such a baby?

<a id="domestic-workers"></a>

### 14.6 Domestic workers

A domestic worker found pregnant before she starts work can be returned to the recruitment office, which must send her home within 10 days <sup>[N81](#n81)</sup>. Domestic workers often hide pregnancies until labour <sup>[N80 (2020)](#n80)</sup>; one woman sent home in 2021 had become pregnant through abuse, was handed to the police and gave birth in a deportation camp <sup>[N61 (2021)](#n61)</sup>. Where sex outside marriage is a crime, migrant women risk prosecution, prison and deportation <sup>[N80 (2020)](#n80)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Who sponsors the residence of a baby born in Oman to a domestic worker?

<a id="sending-money-home"></a>

## 15 Sending money home

<a id="your-bank-account-in-oman-and-the-wage-protection-system"></a>

### 15.1 Your bank account in Oman and the Wage Protection System

Your employer has paid your wage only once it is transferred into your own account at a local bank or financial institution licensed by the Central Bank of Oman (CBO) <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Under the Wage Protection System (WPS, the Ministry of Labour's electronic check that private employers pay wages), the transfer must be made within three days of the end of the wage period <sup>[N82](#n82)</sup>. An employer whose worker has an absconding report approved more than 30 days ago, or whose work has stopped for over 30 days because of a labour dispute, is exempt <sup>[N82](#n82)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which banks open salary accounts for low-wage Bangladeshi workers, with what minimum balance and monthly fee, and does the employer open them or the worker?
> - What happens to a worker's Oman bank account and card after his resident card is cancelled and he leaves?

<a id="send-through-a-bank-or-licensed-exchange-house-never-hundi"></a>

### 15.2 Send through a bank or licensed exchange house, never hundi

Bangladesh's government asks workers to send money only through legal channels, never hundi (informal transfer through unlicensed agents) <sup>[54](#o54)</sup>. Money sent through legal channels earns the receiver a 2.5% government cash incentive, sometimes with a further 2.5% from the bank, and the 2026-27 budget keeps the government incentive <sup>[N83](#n83), [176](#o176)</sup>.

In Oman, nobody may carry out any financial activity without a CBO licence, and money services, including exchange and remittances, are a financial activity under the Banking Law of 2025 <sup>[177](#o177)</sup>. Doing it without a licence carries 3 months to 3 years in jail, a fine of RO 250 to 600 (RO is the Omani rial) for each day of the offence, or both, and the court closes the premises and confiscates the equipment <sup>[177](#o177)</sup>. Police enforce this against people who send money for other workers: in August 2026 Al Buraimi police arrested an Asian national for making international transfers through unlicensed apps for a group of expatriate workers in return for a fee <sup>[N84](#n84)</sup>.

You'll need:
- For a bKash wallet: the receiver's bKash number and full name exactly as registered <sup>[N83](#n83)</sup>.
- For a bank account: your own or a relative's account in Bangladesh <sup>[178](#o178)</sup>.

1. <a id="send-through-a-bank-or-licensed-exchange-house-never-hundi-item-1"></a>**Check the exchange house is licensed.** The CBO's list of money exchange companies (cbo.gov.om/Pages/MoneyExchangeCompanies.aspx) names 17, each with its address and phone: Unimoni, Musandam Exchange, Mustafa Sultan Exchange, Lakhoos Exchange, Oman Exchange, Purshottam Kanji Exchange, Hamdan Exchange, Gulf Overseas Exchange, Modern Exchange, Lulu Exchange, Global Money Exchange, Wasel Exchange, Al Jadeed Exchange, Travelex, Joyalukkas Exchange, Al Amana International Exchange and First Exchange <sup>[179](#o179)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Are "Oman International Exchange" and "Gulf Exchange Oman", which bKash lists as partners, licensed by the CBO under another name, when neither is on the CBO's list <sup>[N83](#n83), [179](#o179)</sup>?

2. <a id="send-through-a-bank-or-licensed-exchange-house-never-hundi-item-2"></a>**Choose the channel.** bKash's partners for sending from Oman are Al Jadeed Exchange, Bank Muscat, Global Money Exchange, Hamdan Exchange, Mustafa Sultan Exchange, Joyalukkas, Purshottam Kanji Exchange, Unimoni, Oman International Exchange, Gulf Exchange Oman, Western Union and MoneyGram <sup>[N83](#n83)</sup>. To send to a Nagad wallet, use a Nagad partner agent, bank or exchange house, including Western Union by agent, app or website; the receiver gets an SMS showing the money and the 2.5% incentive <sup>[N85](#n85)</sup>. To send straight into a bank account, use a bank or exchange house with a "drawing arrangement" with a Bangladeshi bank <sup>[178](#o178)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Which exchange houses in Oman pay into Nagad wallets?

3. <a id="send-through-a-bank-or-licensed-exchange-house-never-hundi-item-3"></a>**Know the cost: small transfers cost more.** Bangladesh Bank's survey put the average cost of sending up to USD 200 from Oman at 6.06% in January-March and 6.77% in April-June 2026, the highest of the ten countries it lists; the average across all amounts sent from Oman was 1.39% and 1.40% <sup>[180](#o180)</sup>. Across all countries that quarter, it cost 2.94% for up to USD 200, 0.94% for USD 201-500 and 0.50% above USD 500 <sup>[180](#o180)</sup>.
4. <a id="send-through-a-bank-or-licensed-exchange-house-never-hundi-item-4"></a>**Send.** For bKash, go to a partner branch or agent with the receiver's bKash number and registered name and hand over the cash; up to Tk 2.5 lakh per transfer, as often as you like <sup>[N83](#n83)</sup>. Banks in Bangladesh credit remittances within 3 days at most <sup>[178](#o178)</sup>.
5. <a id="send-through-a-bank-or-licensed-exchange-house-never-hundi-item-5"></a>**The family collects.** bKash money can be cashed out at BRAC Bank, City Bank and Q-Cash ATM booths for Tk 7 per thousand <sup>[N83](#n83)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does money used to buy Wage Earner Development Bonds, freelancing income, gifts, trade payments or donations get the 2.5% incentive, when a bank's 2025 guide says not <sup>[N86](#n86)</sup>?
> - What does a hundi dealer in Oman pay per rial against the exchange houses' rate in 2026, and how do the "unlicensed apps" police arrest people for work?

**If something goes wrong:**
- bKash helpline: 16247, or +88-02-55663001 from a country not listed <sup>[N83](#n83)</sup>.
- Royal Oman Police (ROP) emergency line: 9999 <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>.

<a id="money-from-oman-by-year"></a>

### 15.3 Money from Oman by year

Workers in Oman sent USD 1,634.7 million home in the 2024-25 financial year and USD 2,048.1 million in 2025-26, up 25.3%, making Oman Bangladesh's seventh-largest source of remittances <sup>[181](#o181)</sup>. All countries together sent USD 30,328.8 million and USD 35,589.4 million <sup>[181](#o181)</sup>. In July-September 2026, Oman sent USD 412.4 million, 5.1% less than a year earlier <sup>[182](#o182)</sup>.

<a id="saving-and-pension-in-bangladesh"></a>

### 15.4 Saving and pension in Bangladesh

<a id="nrcta-non-resident-convertible-taka-account"></a>

#### 15.4.1 NRCTA (Non-Resident Convertible Taka Account)

A taka savings, current or fixed-deposit account for Bangladeshis abroad, funded by remittances, at a scheduled bank's AD (Authorised Dealer, licensed for foreign exchange) branch or Offshore Banking Unit, under Bangladesh Bank's Circular 41 of 8 October 2026 <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>. A worker can open one before departure with no initial deposit, or during a visit home <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>. It earns market interest, and principal and interest can be sent abroad freely <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>. Banks may let someone in Bangladesh operate it under a power of attorney and may send the money to a non-resident nominee if the holder dies, and the account can be kept after coming home for good <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>. Banks may lend in taka against it, including to buy a home for your own use <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>. The trade-off: money sent into an NRCTA generally gets no 2.5% incentive unless specifically stated <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>.

<a id="wage-earner-development-bond-wedb"></a>

#### 15.4.2 Wage Earner Development Bond (WEDB)

A 5-year government bond <sup>[184](#o184)</sup>.
- Sizes: Tk 25,000, 50,000, 1 lakh, 2 lakh, 5 lakh, 10 lakh and 50 lakh <sup>[184](#o184)</sup>.
- Where sold: Bangladesh Bank, any scheduled bank's AD branch, and abroad at branches of Bangladeshi banks, exchange houses and exchange companies <sup>[184](#o184)</sup>.
- Who buys: a legal wage earner, in taka, with remittance sent through legal channels, in his own name or a named person's, with no ceiling <sup>[184](#o184)</sup>.
- Profit at maturity, paid every six months: 12% for holdings up to Tk 15 lakh, 11% for Tk 15-30 lakh, 10% for Tk 30-50 lakh and 9% above <sup>[184](#o184)</sup>.
- Cashing early pays less (up to Tk 15 lakh): 8.70% after 6-12 months, 9.45% after 12-18, 10.20% after 18-24 and 11.20% after 24-60 <sup>[184](#o184)</sup>.
- Other terms: profit and investment are income-tax free; you can name a nominee and borrow against the bond; it has a 40-50% death-risk benefit; and the principal can be taken in US dollars <sup>[184](#o184)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which exchange houses or bank branches in Oman sell the WEDB now?

<a id="the-probash-universal-pension"></a>

#### 15.4.3 The Probash universal pension

The National Pension Authority's Universal Pension "Probash" scheme is for Bangladeshis working or living abroad: you pay in foreign currency and the pension is paid in taka <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>. You join at 18 to 50 with your NID or passport number; over 50 only under special consideration, paying 10 years without a break <sup>[186 (2024)](#o186)</sup>. You pay until 60, and the pension is paid for life <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>. The monthly contribution is Tk 1,000, 2,000, 5,000, 7,500 or 10,000 <sup>[187](#o187)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is the Tk 1,000 contribution still offered, when the scheme's English brief lists only the other four <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>?

**What you get:** On the scheme's estimates, Tk 1,000 a month for 10 years gives about Tk 1,530 a month, and for 30 years about Tk 12,466 <sup>[187](#o187)</sup>. If you die, your nominee gets the pension until you would have turned 75; if you die before paying 10 years, the deposit comes back with profit <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>. You can borrow up to 50% of your deposit, repaid in up to 24 instalments, and the pension is income-tax free <sup>[185](#o185), [188](#o188)</sup>.

**The rules:**

- No lump-sum withdrawal and no leaving before pension age <sup>[188](#o188)</sup>.
- A late contribution has 1 month's grace, then a late fee of 1% a day <sup>[188](#o188)</sup>.
- Never give anyone cash for contributions <sup>[188](#o188)</sup>.
- From Oman you pay in foreign currency, by credit or debit card, through an exchange house or banking channel, or from the Bangladesh account that receives your remittances, not in taka; contributions sent directly in foreign currency get a government incentive <sup>[186 (2024)](#o186), [188](#o188)</sup>.
- Back home you can pay the same amount in taka, or change scheme <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>.
- You can also open a Surakkha account (the scheme for self-employed and informal workers) for a spouse, parent or sibling aged 18 or over in Bangladesh and pay it in foreign currency, with that person's NID, bank account number and nominee details <sup>[187](#o187), [185](#o185)</sup>.

You'll need <sup>[186 (2024)](#o186)</sup>:
- Your NID number (or passport number).
- Your Bangladesh bank account name, number and routing number.
- Your nominee's ID number.

1. <a id="the-probash-universal-pension-item-1"></a>**Register** at www.upension.gov.bd: click Registration, choose Probash, and type your NID or passport number, date of birth, mobile number and the captcha <sup>[186 (2024)](#o186)</sup>. Union Digital Centres (UDC, government service centres at union council level) help with registration <sup>[186 (2024)](#o186)</sup>.
2. <a id="the-probash-universal-pension-item-2"></a>**Fill in** occupation, yearly income, permanent address, bank details, and the nominee's ID number, relationship and share; save, check and submit <sup>[186 (2024)](#o186)</sup>.
3. <a id="the-probash-universal-pension-item-3"></a>**Pay** the first contribution, download the payment slip and keep the email with your pensioner ID <sup>[186 (2024)](#o186)</sup>.
4. <a id="the-probash-universal-pension-item-4"></a>**Set a password** of at least 6 digits and print the pension ID card <sup>[186 (2024)](#o186)</sup>. If you registered with a passport, submit your NID once you have one <sup>[188](#o188)</sup>.

**Help:** National Pension Authority, 43 Kakrail (4 Anjuman Mufidul Islam Road), Dhaka-1000; hotline 16131 or +88 09610 900800 (9 am-5 pm Bangladesh time); support@upension.gov.bd <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>.

<a id="going-home-without-valid-papers"></a>

## 16 Going home without valid papers

A Bangladeshi in Oman without a valid passport goes home on a Travel Permit (an "outpass", a one-way document home) from the Bangladesh Embassy in Muscat, whose Expatriate Service Cell handles out passes alongside body repatriation, legal help, accidents and illness <sup>[N87 (2020)](#n87), [100](#o100), [101](#o101)</sup>.

<a id="what-staying-on-costs-you"></a>

### 16.1 What staying on costs you

- **Overstay fine.** ROP charges RO 20 a day on an expired residence visa and RO 10 a day on an expired visit visa, paid on departure <sup>[115](#o115)</sup>. ROP collects it once you are more than 30 days past the expiry of a residence visa, or more than 24 hours past a visit visa <sup>[114](#o114)</sup>.
- **Criminal penalty.** Failing without an acceptable excuse to apply to renew your residence in time carries 7 days to 3 months in jail, a fine of RO 10 to 500, or both <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>. You must apply to renew 15 days before the residence expires, and renewal can be refused without reasons <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>. Not leaving after being told your renewal was refused carries 1 month to 3 years and RO 100 to 500 <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>. For the late-renewal offence and the others in articles 42 to 45 of the Foreigners' Residence Law, the Inspector General of Police and Customs may drop the case if you pay a settlement of no more than half the maximum fine <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>.
- **Your employer's fines.** The employer pays the Ministry of Labour RO 10 a month for a late work-permit renewal, and RO 15 (an individual employer) or RO 20 (a company) a month for not correcting a worker's status, each capped at RO 500 per worker <sup>[N31](#n31), [111](#o111)</sup>. The status-correction fine runs until the worker leaves, is transferred, is reported absent, his death is registered or his visa changed <sup>[N31](#n31)</sup>. No fines run while a complaint for his dues, filed after the contract ended, awaits final judgment, while his passport is held by a government body or his embassy, or while he is in prison under a sentence <sup>[N31](#n31), [111](#o111)</sup>.
- **Lost passport.** Report it at once to the nearest police station, and go to the competent authority yourself or through your sponsor within 24 hours; your residence ends if no replacement is shown within three months <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>.
- **Kept from leaving.** A foreigner under an enforceable judgment, an arrest order or a court travel ban cannot leave until it is settled, and a creditor holding a court judgment can ask for a travel ban until the debt is paid <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is the RO 20 a day on an expired residence visa capped, and how does it relate to ROP's RO 50 fine for a late residence-permit renewal <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>?
> - Can a worker whose card expired months ago transfer to a new employer without leaving Oman, outside an amnesty?

<a id="amnesties-and-grace-periods"></a>

### 16.2 Amnesties and grace periods

The Ministry of Labour's last grace period ran from 1 February 2025 and let people renew labour cards, cancel absconding ("work abandonment") reports, transfer workers' services and settle repatriation-ticket costs without penalties <sup>[N88](#n88)</sup>. It waived fines and debts worth more than RO 60 million, including fines on labour cards expired and unused for over seven years and repatriation-ticket debts from 2017 and earlier <sup>[N88](#n88)</sup>. Applications went through the Ministry of Labour's website and its approved service channels <sup>[N88](#n88)</sup>. In the 2020 window, workers registered on the Ministry's website or at the nearest Sanad office (licensed government-services counters) <sup>[N87 (2020)](#n87)</sup>. Fines were waived only on condition of renewing the work permit and paying the next period's fees, cancelling the worker's service, transferring services, or paying the worker's repatriation ticket <sup>[N89](#n89)</sup>. ROP ran a matching window: residents could renew residence or transfer employment without penalties for expired entry or residence permits, after Ministry of Labour verification, and those leaving Oman for good were exempted from fines on expired non-work visas <sup>[N90](#n90)</sup>. The deadline, first 31 July 2025, was extended to 31 December 2025 as an "additional and final" grace period, with legal and administrative consequences promised after it <sup>[N91](#n91), [N90](#n90)</sup>. No requests after December 2025 were to be considered <sup>[N89](#n89), [N53](#n53)</sup>. The same initiative cancelled work cards inactive for more than 10 years with no service requests <sup>[N89](#n89)</sup>. In February 2026 Oman's labour minister said about RO 100 million in labour fines had been waived during 2025 <sup>[N92](#n92)</sup>. In January 2026 Bangladesh's expatriates' welfare adviser praised Oman's move to regularise undocumented Bangladeshis without fines <sup>[N5](#n5)</sup>.

During the 2025 window, the Embassy planned a consular tour to Salalah for priority passport enrolment, with sign-up through an online form and a published serial list <sup>[N93](#n93)</sup>.

Fake amnesty news circulates: a Bangla news site on 16 September 2026 ran an "Oman" amnesty with a 1 October deadline whose text in fact describes ASEZA, the authority of Jordan's Aqaba zone, and people leaving Jordan <sup>[N94](#n94)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Has the Ministry of Labour or ROP opened, or signalled, any new grace period or fines waiver since 31 December 2025?
> - In the 2025 window, what did a worker with no passport, or an approved absconding report, have to do step by step at a Sanad office?

<a id="help-from-bangladesh"></a>

### 16.3 Help from Bangladesh

A close relative in Bangladesh can ask the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare to help bring a worker home; the Ministry acts through the Embassy, WEWB (the Wage Earners' Welfare Board) or BMET, free, within 7 working days of receiving the letter <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>. The relative gives <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>:
- Proof that the person is a migrant worker.
- The workplace and residence address in Oman.
- The recruiting agency's name and address.
- The employer's mobile number.

<a id="step-1-get-a-passport-or-travel-permit"></a>

### 16.4 Step 1: Get a passport or Travel Permit

In the 2020 exit window, the Embassy said a worker with a valid passport needed no Travel Permit, and one with an expired passport could renew it at the Embassy instead <sup>[N87 (2020)](#n87)</sup>. A labourer's new 48-page, 5-year passport at the Embassy costs RO 12.700, or RO 19.100 urgent <sup>[175](#o175)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-1-get-a-passport-or-travel-permit-item-1"></a>**Book.** Every Embassy service is booked: pay any fee first at Bank Muscat, then fill in the online form (forms.gle/CYHtStaaKWC1afwN6, also through amardutabashmuscat.org) and come on the schedule and serial number the Embassy publishes <sup>[81](#o81), [164](#o164)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-1-get-a-passport-or-travel-permit-item-2"></a>**Pay by bank, never cash.** Since 1 December 2025 the Embassy takes no cash <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>. Pay at any Bank Muscat branch or by mobile app into the account for the service, writing the main part of your name and your passport number or OID (Omani resident card number) in the reference, and keep the yellow receipt <sup>[81](#o81), [165](#o165)</sup>. A wrong account or wrong amount causes serious trouble <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-1-get-a-passport-or-travel-permit-item-3"></a>**Fill in the Travel Permit form** and submit it at the Embassy; collect the permit on the date given (the 2020 procedure) <sup>[N87 (2020)](#n87)</sup>. In 2020 the Embassy said the Travel Permit service was completely free and warned against paying any middleman or broker <sup>[N87 (2020)](#n87)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the Travel Permit fee now, when the Embassy's fee lists of March and June 2026 give none <sup>[165](#o165), [175](#o175)</sup>?
> - What documents does the Embassy want for a Travel Permit (passport copy, photos, police report, letter from the employer or ROP), and how many days does it take?

**If it fails:**
- Expatriate Service Cell, 24 hours, by WhatsApp or Facebook text or voice message only: +968 91980621; consularmuscat@gmail.com <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>.
- Passport and visa landline, 8:30 am-4:30 pm: 24698660; Counsellor (Passport and Visa), +968 24698098, fs.passportandvisa.muscat@ssd.gov.bd <sup>[100](#o100), [80](#o80)</sup>.

<a id="step-2-pay-the-fines-and-fly"></a>

### 16.5 Step 2: Pay the fines and fly

1. <a id="step-2-pay-the-fines-and-fly-item-1"></a>**Settle the work permit with the Ministry of Labour.** In the 2025 window, work-card fines were waived only if the permit was renewed with the next period's fees, the worker's service was cancelled or transferred, or his repatriation ticket was paid <sup>[N89](#n89)</sup>; outside a window, the employer's late fines stand ([What staying on costs you](#what-staying-on-costs-you)) <sup>[N31](#n31)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-pay-the-fines-and-fly-item-2"></a>**Pay the ROP exit fine online** before the airport: fill in ROP's Online Exit Penalty Payment form, pay online, print the receipt and show it at the exit desk; the payment is not refunded, and ROP can reject the form without giving reasons <sup>[114](#o114)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-2-pay-the-fines-and-fly-item-3"></a>**Fly.** In the 2020 exit scheme, leavers reported to the Ministry of Labour counter at Muscat International Airport 7 hours before the flight with the ticket and the passport or Embassy-signed Travel Permit <sup>[N87 (2020)](#n87)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-2-pay-the-fines-and-fly-item-4"></a>**The ticket.** At the end of a contract, the employer must send the worker home within 60 days <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>; if the worker refuses to go, the Ministry sends him at its own cost and recovers it from the employer <sup>[N29 (2023)](#n29)</sup>. A worker who sues for his dues may stay in Oman until the case is decided <sup>[N29 (2023)](#n29)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Who pays the ticket of an undocumented worker who has no employer to claim from: the worker, the Embassy, WEWB or Oman?

<a id="if-you-are-detained"></a>

### 16.6 If you are detained

A foreigner ordered deported is told to leave by his own means within the time in the order; if he does not, he is removed by force and can be held for up to two weeks to carry it out <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>. If he has affairs to settle, he gets one month, renewable, against a personal guarantee <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>. A foreigner who entered illegally is removed at his own cost or the cost of whoever brought him in or employed him <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>, and a worker employed without authorisation is deported at the employing party's expense <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. A court can order deportation for any offence under the residence law <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Where are Oman's deportation holding centres, can the Embassy visit them, and how long do Bangladeshis wait there in 2026?

<a id="bans-after-you-leave"></a>

### 16.7 Bans after you leave

A foreigner who was deported can be given an entry visa only after two years, and then only with the special permission of the Inspector General of Police and Customs <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>. Anyone removed or deported is put on the list of people not wanted in Oman <sup>[N62 (2021)](#n62)</sup>. A worker caught working without authorisation is deported and barred from entering Oman <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does a worker who pays his overstay fine and leaves on his own, without a deportation order, face any ban on coming back?

<a id="if-a-worker-dies-is-injured-or-loses-the-job"></a>

## 17 If a worker dies, is injured or loses the job

WEWB pays grants and helps with the migrant insurance for workers who left with BMET clearance or joined the Board <sup>[189](#o189), [93 (2024)](#o93)</sup>. The Embassy's Expatriate Service Cell handles body repatriation, legal help, accidents and illness <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>. Bangladesh's labour wings recovered Tk 127.6 crore (1 crore is Tk 10 million) in 1,852 cases of death compensation, wages and benefits in 2023-24 <sup>[92 (2024)](#o92)</sup>.

Of 17,871 bodies of migrant workers brought home from July 2016 to June 2022, 1,893 came from Oman, against 5,666 from Saudi Arabia and 1,913 from the UAE <sup>[N95](#n95)</sup>. For comparison, 387 Indian nationals died in Oman in 2024 <sup>[22 (2024)](#o22)</sup>. From all countries, a record 5,038 bodies came home in 2025, after 4,813 in 2024 <sup>[N96](#n96), [N95](#n95)</sup>, and 4,060 from January to September 2026, more than 14 a day <sup>[N97](#n97)</sup>. In the first nine months of 2026, 66% came with "natural death" written as the cause <sup>[N97](#n97)</sup>. A regular worker's body comes home roughly on time, but an irregular worker's takes much longer <sup>[N97](#n97)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How many Bangladeshi workers died in Oman each year from 2023 to 2026, and of what causes?

<a id="check-you-are-covered"></a>

### 17.1 Check you are covered

WEWB's death grant needs BMET emigration clearance or Board membership, and death in the country the clearance was for <sup>[189](#o189)</sup>. Workers with a BMET clearance card are members automatically; anyone can check at portal.wewb.gov.bd/bmet-wewbm-check <sup>[90](#o90)</sup>. A worker in Oman without clearance can join online at portal.wewb.gov.bd <sup>[90](#o90)</sup>; at the Embassy in Muscat, membership registration costs RO 22, paid into the Embassy's Welfare Account at Bank Muscat (0316006442800014) and booked like any other service <sup>[165](#o165)</sup>. Workers cleared on or after 12 March 2023 also have the JBC (Jiban Bima Corporation, the state life insurer) migrant insurance <sup>[54](#o54), [93 (2024)](#o93)</sup>.

<a id="if-you-are-injured-or-fall-ill-1"></a>

### 17.2 If you are injured or fall ill

1. <a id="if-you-are-injured-or-fall-ill-1-item-1"></a>**Treatment in Oman.** If you are treated in a government or private hospital without health insurance cover, the employer pays for treatment, medicine and the hospital stay <sup>[8](#o8), [N29 (2023)](#n29)</sup>. Work-injury insurance under the Social Protection Law does not yet cover non-Omani workers: Royal Decree 60/2025 moved its start to five years after the law's issuing decree of July 2023 <sup>[107](#o107)</sup>, which advisers date to July 2028 <sup>[N46](#n46)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Until the work-injury insurance starts, what compensation does a worker injured at work in Oman get from the employer, and how does he claim it?

2. <a id="if-you-are-injured-or-fall-ill-1-item-2"></a>**Report it.** In an emergency call 9999 <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>. For an accident that has just happened, the Embassy's hotline is +968 91997852 (voice calls, 24 hours) <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-you-are-injured-or-fall-ill-1-item-3"></a>**Going home sick or injured.** On the worker's or family's application, with proof of clearance or membership and a passport copy, WEWB asks the mission to bring the worker home and in special cases helps with hospital admission <sup>[190](#o190)</sup>. A free ambulance from Dhaka and Chattogram airports takes injured or sick returnees on request <sup>[191](#o191)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-you-are-injured-or-fall-ill-1-item-4"></a>**Treatment aid in Bangladesh.** Returnees with clearance or membership can get up to Tk 2 lakh for treatment; apply within 1 year of return with medical reports and prescriptions, a bank statement, a copy of the arrival stamp and the passport <sup>[192](#o192)</sup>.
5. <a id="if-you-are-injured-or-fall-ill-1-item-5"></a>**The insurance.** It pays 100% of Tk 10 lakh for total permanent disability, and for partial disability <sup>[93 (2024)](#o93), [54](#o54)</sup>:
   - 90%: whole arm or leg.
   - 75%: arm at the elbow or leg below the knee.
   - 50%: one eye or one hand at the wrist.
   - 25%: lower jaw, or four fingers with thumb.
   - 15%: thumb, index or middle finger, or all toes.
   - 10%: ring or little finger, the big toe, any one other toe, or four toes including the big toe.

   If the worker later dies, the death claim pays the sum insured minus what was paid for the partial disability <sup>[93 (2024)](#o93)</sup>. Claims go through WEWB to JBC, as in If a worker dies, step 8 <sup>[93 (2024)](#o93)</sup>.

<a id="if-a-worker-dies"></a>

### 17.3 If a worker dies

1. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-item-1"></a>**The sponsor reports the death.** Under Omani labour law the sponsor (the employer holding the worker's visa) is responsible for the body <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>. The sponsor tells the nearest police station and gets a Death Notification from the hospital, on which ROP's civil registration issues the death certificate <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>. In Muscat, ROP's Directorate General of Civil Status is across from the airport, beside Al Azaiba Police Station <sup>[194 (2024)](#o194)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-item-2"></a>**The Embassy's NOC.** The body needs the deceased's embassy's no-objection letter (NOC) for autopsy, embalming, repatriation or local burial <sup>[194 (2024)](#o194)</sup>. Contact the Embassy's labour wing or the Expatriate Service Cell ([Contacts](#contacts)) <sup>[100](#o100), [80](#o80)</sup>. Death registration at the Embassy costs RO 2 <sup>[165](#o165)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What papers does the Bangladesh Embassy in Muscat want before it issues the NOC, and how long does it take?

3. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-item-3"></a>**Post-mortem.** Post-mortem is compulsory in suicide and murder cases <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>. Salalah has no post-mortem facility, so ROP there asks the next of kin for a no-objection letter without post-mortem, and a family wanting one must have the body taken to Muscat <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-item-4"></a>**Release, certificate and embalming.** With the NOC and the death notification, the sponsor goes to the police station for the body's release, then to Civil Status for the death certificate <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>. The documents (death certificate, NOC, hospital notification, a certificate of no infectious disease, a coffin contents and sealing certificate, and the passport) go with the body, in an original and 12 copies with the cancelled passport and the airway bill; the employer or family provides transport and handlers <sup>[194 (2024)](#o194)</sup>. The sponsor takes the papers to the cargo office at Seeb <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>.
5. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-item-5"></a>**The cost.** Embalming costs RO 123 at the ROP hospital or RO 100 at Khoula Hospital, airport cargo handling about RO 77, and a casket RO 180 to 200 <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>. WEWB says a body usually comes home at the employer's cost; where no employer is found, it refuses, or the community can't help, WEWB pays from money it gives the labour wings each year <sup>[92 (2024)](#o92)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What does air freight of a body from Muscat or Salalah to Dhaka or Sylhet cost in 2026, and who pays it in practice for a worker whose sponsor has disappeared?

6. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-item-6"></a>**Burial in Oman instead.** The family must give a no-objection letter for local burial <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>. WEWB takes the family's choice (body home or burial in Oman) to the mission and follows the case until the body arrives, free <sup>[195](#o195), [196](#o196)</sup>.
7. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-item-7"></a>**The family in Bangladesh can ask for help.** The family has to apply; if the worker left with BMET clearance or joined the Board through the mission, the Board pays to bring the body home, and if not, the family can still apply and the Board passes it to the Ministry, which pays from a yearly budget <sup>[N97](#n97)</sup>. The Ministry also brings home the bodies of irregular, undocumented and destitute workers: the next of kin applies with what happened, the cause of death, proof of status (passport, work permit, smart card or foreign ID) and the death certificate; free, 7 working days <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>. Probash-Bondhu: 16135, +8809610102030 from abroad <sup>[59](#o59)</sup>.
8. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-item-8"></a>**At the airport: Tk 35,000.** For every body that arrives, the airport welfare desk gives the family a Tk 35,000 cheque for transport and burial, free and at once, collectable within 3 months <sup>[197](#o197), [198](#o198)</sup>. A free ambulance from Dhaka and Chattogram airports is available on request <sup>[191](#o191)</sup>. You'll need <sup>[198](#o198), [197](#o197), [49](#o49)</sup>:
   - The deceased's passport copy.
   - A heirship certificate from the local chairman or commissioner.
   - The NID of the wife, father or mother.
   - The death certificate.
   - The NOC.
   - The original airway bill.
   - BMET clearance or membership proof, if any.
   - A photo and NID or passport copy of the nominated family member (and of the collector if different).
9. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-item-9"></a>**The Tk 3 lakh grant.** If the family took the airport cheque, no separate application is needed; if the body was buried in Oman, the family applies within 1 year of the death to WEWB's Director General or the district welfare centre <sup>[189](#o189)</sup>. It goes to the spouse, children and parents; for an unmarried worker with no living parents, to full siblings <sup>[189](#o189)</sup>. It is paid into the family's bank account by BEFTN (electronic bank transfer), with an SMS <sup>[189](#o189)</sup>. The family applies with <sup>[189](#o189)</sup>:
   - A family-members certificate from the UP (Union Parishad, the union council) chairman, mayor or city councillor, countersigned by the UNO (Upazila Nirbahi Officer, the sub-district's chief officer).
   - An undertaking on Tk 400 non-judicial stamp paper.
   - The Embassy NOC or the death certificate.
   - A bank statement with the routing number.
   - Attested colour photos of each family member.
   - The deceased's passport copy.
10. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-item-10"></a>**The insurance: Tk 10 lakh.** It pays 100% for natural death, and for accidental death during the cover or within 90 days after it ends <sup>[93 (2024)](#o93)</sup>. WEWB checks the claim papers and recommends to JBC, which pays WEWB <sup>[93 (2024)](#o93)</sup>; WEWB helps the family claim with proof of clearance or membership and a passport copy, free, on 16135 <sup>[199](#o199)</sup>.

    > **Need to know**
    > - What are JBC's official claim form, document list and exclusions <sup>[93 (2024)](#o93)</sup>?
    > - Must the family apply for the death payment, or does it reach the worker's bank account without an application, as a 2024 news report says <sup>[N98 (2024)](#n98)</sup>?

11. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-item-11"></a>**Money owed in Oman.** For a road accident there is no post-mortem, and compensation or blood money (diya, paid under Islamic law) is pursued through lawyers, for which the family sends an original power of attorney and a legal-heir certificate <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>. WEWB's legal cell helps the heirs prepare the power of attorney; the family gives a family-members certificate, a Tk 400 non-judicial stamp undertaking, a bank statement with routing number and 2 colour photos of each heir <sup>[200](#o200)</sup>. The Embassy authenticates a power of attorney for RO 3.900 for the first 5 pages <sup>[165](#o165)</sup>. The next of kin can also apply to the Ministry at aswelfare@probashi.gov.bd with proof of status, employer details and amounts owed; free; the Ministry writes to the mission and WEWB within 7 working days <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>.

    > **Need to know**
    > - Does the Bangladesh Embassy in Muscat have empanelled lawyers for death compensation and blood-money cases, and what do they charge?

<a id="if-you-lose-the-job"></a>

### 17.4 If you lose the job

1. <a id="if-you-lose-the-job-item-1"></a>**In Oman,** the employer must send you home within 60 days of the contract ending <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. To claim unpaid wages or dues, file a free complaint on the Ministry of Labour's "Record Labor Complaint" service on gov.om; it goes to a meeting of both parties, then to a judge and the court <sup>[108](#o108)</sup>. You may stay in Oman while your court case is decided <sup>[N29 (2023)](#n29)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-you-lose-the-job-item-2"></a>**From Bangladesh,** you or your family can apply to the Ministry about unpaid wages, allowances or insurance, with proof of status, the employer's name and address and the amounts owed; the Ministry writes to the mission or WEWB within 7 working days, free <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-you-lose-the-job-item-3"></a>**Job-loss insurance.** Tk 50,000 if you lose the job and return within 6 months of going, but not if you went entirely at the employer's cost <sup>[93 (2024)](#o93), [54](#o54)</sup>. Claim through WEWB with proof of clearance or membership and a passport copy; free <sup>[199](#o199)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-you-lose-the-job-item-4"></a>**If you can't get home,** a relative in Bangladesh can ask the Ministry for help ([Going home without valid papers](#going-home-without-valid-papers)) <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>.

**If a claim stalls:**
