- Probash-Bondhu: 16135, +8809610102030 from abroad <sup>[59](#o59)</sup>.
- JBC Group Insurance: 02-223381423, gid@jbc.gov.bd <sup>[93 (2024)](#o93)</sup>.
- WEWB complaints focal person: 02-222220976, ad.pro@wewb.gov.bd <sup>[92 (2024)](#o92)</sup>.

<a id="coming-home"></a>

## 18 Coming home

At Dhaka, Chattogram and Sylhet airports, WEWB's Probashi Kallyan (expatriate welfare) desks meet returning workers <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>.

1. <a id="coming-home-item-1"></a>**Leaving Oman with cash or gold.** Declare cash, bearer securities, precious metals or gems worth RO 6,000 or more to Oman Customs on its declaration form; not declaring is an offence under the anti-money-laundering law <sup>[201](#o201)</sup>.
2. <a id="coming-home-item-2"></a>**At the airport in Bangladesh.** The welfare desks help with customs and lost luggage, send sick returnees to hospital by ambulance, arrange a stay at the Wage Earners' Centre, and take complaints from abused or cheated returnees <sup>[198](#o198)</sup>. Women back within 1 year after abuse or fraud get Tk 5,000 in cash, and workers back because of war, disaster or lay-off can get financial help <sup>[49](#o49), [198](#o198)</sup>. Desks:
   - Dhaka: +88 02 8901040, mobile +880 1971 430203, welfaredesk@gmail.com <sup>[198](#o198)</sup>.
   - Chattogram: +880 2333300957, mobile +880 1912542185, pkd.chattogram@wewb.gov.bd <sup>[198](#o198)</sup>.
   - Sylhet: 01321158330, mobile 01973-898375, pkdsylhet@gmail.com <sup>[198](#o198)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Which desk numbers answer now, given that BMET's citizen charter gives Chattogram 01913631797, pkdctg@gmail.com, and Sylhet 01558277904 <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>, and WEWB's ambulance page gives Dhaka 01404 413388 <sup>[191](#o191)</sup>?
3. <a id="coming-home-item-3"></a>**A place to stay.** The Wage Earners' Centre near Shahjalal airport (Lonjonipara, Borua, Khilkhet, Dhaka-1229): book at wec.wewb.gov.bd or in person with clearance or membership proof, a passport copy and an air ticket copy; Tk 100 admission and Tk 200 a night; 01310 350555, 01754 715720 <sup>[202](#o202)</sup>. The Probashi Lounge at Shahjalal airport gives free wifi, telephone, TV and printing, and food at up to 30% off, on clearance or membership proof <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>.

<a id="what-you-can-bring-home"></a>

### 18.1 What you can bring home

Bangladesh's Non-tourist Passenger Baggage Rules 2025 (SRO 261 of the National Board of Revenue, gazetted 3 June 2025) set what a returning migrant worker may bring <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>.

<a id="duty-free"></a>

#### 18.1.1 Duty-free

- Baggage: a passenger aged 12 or over may bring up to 65 kg free of all duty and tax, plus up to 35 kg more of clothes, personal items, books and study materials <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>.
- One of each <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>: a desktop or laptop (with one UPS, an uninterruptible power supply), printer, scanner, ordinary camera, oven or microwave, rice or pressure cooker, blender or juicer, sewing machine, fan, sports equipment, a TV up to 29 inches, a music system, one carpet up to 15 sq m and 1 carton (200) of cigarettes.
- Phones: 2 used mobile phones and 1 new one, the new one only once a year <sup>[204](#o204), [205](#o205)</sup>.
- Jewellery: declared gold and silver jewellery, once a year, no more than 12 pieces of one type <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is the jewellery allowance 100 g of gold or 200 g of silver in total, or 100 g of gold and 200 g of silver, when the Rules say both <sup>[204](#o204), [205](#o205)</sup>?

<a id="with-duty"></a>

#### 18.1.2 With duty

- Gold or silver bar, once a year, declared: one gold bar of up to 117 g (10 tola), at Tk 5,000 duty per 11.664 g, or a silver bar of up to 234 g (20 tola); anything over, or a hidden bar, is confiscated, and an undeclared bar can lead to arrest <sup>[204](#o204), [205](#o205)</sup>.
- Bigger items, one of each <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>:
  - TV 30-36 inches: Tk 10,000 (37-42 inches: Tk 20,000).
  - Refrigerator or deep freezer: Tk 5,000.
  - Window air conditioner: Tk 7,000.
  - Split AC up to 18,000 BTU (cooling capacity): Tk 15,000.
  - Washing machine or dishwasher: Tk 3,000.

<a id="not-allowed"></a>

#### 18.1.3 Not allowed

A Bangladeshi passport holder may not bring in any alcohol <sup>[204](#o204), [205](#o205)</sup>.

<a id="declaring"></a>

#### 18.1.4 Declaring

Every arriving passenger fills in the baggage declaration form (Schedule 1) or submits it online; with nothing dutiable you use the green channel, where up to 5% of passengers' bags are scanned at random <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>. If you missed it, you can still declare on the Schedule 1 form to a customs officer of at least Assistant Commissioner rank within 30 days of arrival <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does Dhaka airport customs accept a late declaration after 7 days, when its page sets 7 days for declaring at the custom house next to the airport <sup>[205](#o205)</sup>?

<a id="money"></a>

#### 18.1.5 Money

Up to USD 10,000 in foreign currency comes in without declaring, and that amount can be kept and taken out again when you go abroad; above it, declare on the FMJ (Foreign Money and Jewellery) form <sup>[206 (2020)](#o206), [205](#o205)</sup>. Customs emergency line: +88 01319-056591 <sup>[205](#o205)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Do customs officers still apply USD 5,000, the figure on Bangladesh Customs' own Currency Regulations page <sup>[207](#o207)</sup>?

<a id="support-after-return"></a>

### 18.2 Support after return

1. <a id="support-after-return-item-1"></a>**PKB rehabilitation loan.** Returnees starting a project can borrow up to Tk 50 lakh from PKB (Probashi Kallyan Bank, the state bank for migrants): up to Tk 3 lakh without collateral, Tk 3-5 lakh with collateral, above Tk 5 lakh against a registered mortgage; up to 10 years at 9% simple interest; decided within 15 working days <sup>[208](#o208)</sup>. Fees: 0.50% processing and 0.50% documentation, each at most Tk 15,000; no service charge <sup>[208](#o208)</sup>. In 2021 only about 2% of pandemic returnees got PKB's special reintegration loan; a study blamed lack of staff, poor promotion and a short grace period <sup>[N99 (2021)](#n99)</sup>, and a returnee said its many conditions put him off <sup>[N99 (2021)](#n99)</sup>.

   **You'll need** <sup>[208](#o208)</sup>:
   - The bank's free application form, at the branch in the project area.
   - 3 photos, passport, NID copy and an address certificate from the municipality or union council.
   - A guarantor (any person able to repay, such as a relative) with 2 photos, passport or NID copy, address certificate and a clean CIB report (Bangladesh Bank's credit record showing no unpaid loans).
   - An updated trade licence (compulsory).
   - Project details with the last 2 years' income and expense statement.
   - The lease papers with a letter of disclaimer, or proof of ownership, for the project site.
   - A declaration of your own investment.
   - Copies of the collateral property papers.
   - Copies of all papers on your return from abroad.
   - Any training or experience certificates.
   - A declaration of other loans.
   - 3 signed MICR cheque leaves from an active account in the business's name, and 3 from one guarantor's account with its statement.
2. <a id="support-after-return-item-2"></a>**WEWB reintegration help.** WEWB's World Bank-funded RAISE (Recovery and Advancement of Informal Sector Employment) project ran from July 2021 to June 2026, giving returnees cash incentives, help with self-employment and loans, counselling and training through district welfare centres <sup>[209](#o209), [92 (2024)](#o92)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Which RAISE services continue after June 2026 <sup>[209](#o209), [92 (2024)](#o92)</sup>?
3. <a id="support-after-return-item-3"></a>**BRAC Migration Programme.** BRAC, a development organisation founded in Bangladesh in 1972, runs a Migration Programme that gives returnees airport help, counselling, help to start earning, and legal assistance <sup>[210](#o210), [211](#o211)</sup>.
4. <a id="support-after-return-item-4"></a>**Children's scholarships.** WEWB pays scholarships to migrants' high-achieving children (Tk 27,500 a year at SSC level, Tk 34,000 at HSC level), and reserves 0.5% of HSC admission seats for them <sup>[92 (2024)](#o92)</sup>.
5. <a id="support-after-return-item-5"></a>**If your family is threatened** or its property seized, apply to WEWB directly or through the expatriate welfare branch of the DC's office or the DEMO (District Employment and Manpower Office); WEWB writes to the local administration, such as the Deputy Commissioner or Superintendent of Police <sup>[212](#o212), [92 (2024)](#o92)</sup>.
6. <a id="support-after-return-item-6"></a>**Savings.** An NRCTA can be kept after coming home for good, and Probash pension contributions can continue in taka ([Sending money home](#sending-money-home)) <sup>[183](#o183), [185](#o185)</sup>.

<a id="contacts"></a>

## 19 Contacts

Switchboard +968 2469 8336, also +968 24698098 and +968 24634798; fax +968 2469 8789; mission.muscat@mofa.gov.bd; muscat.mofa.gov.bd <sup>[100](#o100), [101](#o101)</sup>. Open Sunday-Thursday 8:00 am-4:00 pm; Friday closed; Saturday closed except passport delivery <sup>[164](#o164), [213 (2023)](#o213)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**

- **Labour wing:** landline for workers' problems +968 24603514, 8:30 am-4:30 pm <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>; Counsellor (Labour), +968 24698440, clomanbd@mail.com <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>; First Secretary (Labour), +968 24698440, ssmuscat@probashi.gov.bd <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>; the Chargé d'affaires is the Minister (Labour), +968 24698440, ministermuscat@probashi.gov.bd <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>.
- **Passport and visa wing:** 24698660 <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>; Counsellor (Passport and Visa), +968 24698098, fs.passportandvisa.muscat@ssd.gov.bd <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>.

It is not an executive authority: it tries to settle cases with the agencies and gives a reference number to track the case <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>.

> **Need to know**

<a id="omans-offices-in-bangladesh"></a>

### 19.2 Oman's offices in Bangladesh

- **Embassy of the Sultanate of Oman, Dhaka:** Plot No. 1, Road No. 68, Gulshan 2, Dhaka; +880 2-984 0398, +880 2-984 0332; dhaka@fm.gov.om <sup>[44](#o44)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does Oman use a visa application centre in Dhaka, and what are the Oman Embassy's consular hours?

<a id="in-bangladesh"></a>

### 19.3 In Bangladesh

- **Probash-Bondhu call centre (WEWB), 24/7:** 16135 toll free in Bangladesh, +8809610102030 from abroad <sup>[59](#o59)</sup>.
- **WEWB:** membership check portal.wewb.gov.bd/bmet-wewbm-check <sup>[90](#o90)</sup>; complaints focal person, 02-222220976, ad.pro@wewb.gov.bd <sup>[92 (2024)](#o92)</sup>.

  - Agency complaints (enforcement branch): sasenforcement@probashi.gov.bd, 02-41030255 <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>.
  - Grievance officer: 02-41030224, 01712992086, adsadmin@probashi.gov.bd <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>.
- **BMET:** Director (Emigration), +8802222222415, 01711-135901, diremi@bmet.gov.bd <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>; complaints against agencies by application with proof to the Director General, free, 30 working days <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>. OEP hotline 09614445271 <sup>[58](#o58)</sup>.
- **PKB:** helpline 16238, from abroad +880 9677-787878 <sup>[94](#o94)</sup>; loans department 01716-175560, migration@pkb.gov.bd <sup>[97](#o97)</sup>.
- **JBC (migrant insurance):** Group Insurance Division, 24 Motijheel C/A, Dhaka-1000; 02-223381423, gid@jbc.gov.bd <sup>[93 (2024)](#o93)</sup>.
- **Airport welfare desks:**
  - Dhaka: +88 02 8901040, +880 1971 430203, welfaredesk@gmail.com <sup>[198](#o198)</sup>.
  - Chattogram: +880 2333300957, +880 1912542185, pkd.chattogram@wewb.gov.bd <sup>[198](#o198)</sup>.
  - Sylhet: 01321158330, 01973-898375, pkdsylhet@gmail.com <sup>[198](#o198)</sup>.
